<?php
/* ===========================================================
   Websitemodel 1 - Admin (FormForge)
   Gebaseerd op de admin van generaalprojecten.nl, maar neutraal:
   alles wat per klant verschilt staat in Instellingen.

   - Eerste bezoek: e-mailadres en wachtwoord instellen (data/config.json)
   - Instellingen (bedrijfsgegevens, teksten, diensten, kleuren, logo): data/settings.json
   - Projecten: data/pages.json, elk project een eigen map /{slug}/index.html
   - Foto's: /uploads/{slug}/, logo: /uploads/site/
   - De admin maakt zelf: index.html, dienstpagina's, /contact/, /bedankt/,
     site.json, sitemap.xml en robots.txt
   - Wachtwoord kwijt: verwijder data/config.json, dan verschijnt het
     instelscherm opnieuw (projecten en instellingen blijven bewaard)
   =========================================================== */

function load_settings() {
    $s = default_settings();
    if (file_exists(SETTINGS_FILE)) {
        $d = json_decode(file_get_contents(SETTINGS_FILE), true);
        if (is_array($d)) { $s = array_merge($s, $d); }
    }
    return $s;
}
function save_settings($s) {
    ensure_data_dir();
    file_put_contents(SETTINGS_FILE, json_encode($s, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES), LOCK_EX);
}
$ST = load_settings();

function st($k) { global $ST; return trim((string)($ST[$k] ?? '')); }

/* Actieve diensten: sleutel => gegevens */
function services() {
    global $ST;
    $out = [];
    foreach (($ST['diensten'] ?? []) as $d) {
        if (trim($d['naam'] ?? '') === '' || empty($d['key']) || empty($d['slug'])) continue;
        $out[$d['key']] = $d;
    }
    return $out;
}
function cat_names() {
    $o = [];
    foreach (services() as $k => $d) { $o[$k] = $d['naam']; }
    return $o;
}
function service_url($key) {
    $s = services();
    return isset($s[$key]) ? '/' . $s[$key]['slug'] . '/' : '/#diensten';
}

/* Webadres van de site: uit de instellingen, anders het huidige domein */
function site_url() {
    $u = st('site_url');
    if ($u !== '') return rtrim($u, '/');
    $host = preg_replace('/[^a-zA-Z0-9.\-:]/', '', (string)($_SERVER['HTTP_HOST'] ?? 'localhost'));
    $https = (!empty($_SERVER['HTTPS']) && $_SERVER['HTTPS'] !== 'off') || (($_SERVER['HTTP_X_FORWARDED_PROTO'] ?? '') === 'https');
    return ($https ? 'https://' : 'http://') . $host;
}
function tel_link($t) {
    $d = preg_replace('/[^0-9+]/', '', (string)$t);
    if ($d === '') return '';
    if (strpos($d, '00') === 0) return '+' . substr($d, 2);
    if ($d[0] === '0') return '+31' . substr($d, 1);
    return $d;
}
function valid_color($c, $fallback) {
    return preg_match('/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/', (string)$c) ? strtolower($c) : $fallback;
}
function logo_url() {
    $l = st('logo');
    return $l !== '' ? '/uploads/site/' . rawurlencode($l) : '';
}
function chat_url() {
    $c = st('chat_url');
    return preg_match('#^https://#i', $c) ? $c : '';
}

/* ---------- Stijlen van de FormForge-server ----------
   Nieuwe stijlen zet FormForge centraal neer. De admin haalt de lijst
   hooguit twee keer per dag op en bewaart hem. Lukt dat niet, dan gelden
   de laatst opgehaalde stijlen, en anders de vijf ingebouwde. */
define('STIJLEN_CACHE', DATA_DIR . '/stijlen-cache.json');
function stijl_http_get($url) {
    if (function_exists('ff_http_get')) return ff_http_get($url, 5);
    if (function_exists('curl_init')) {
        $ch = curl_init($url);
        curl_setopt_array($ch, [CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 5, CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => 5]);
        $b = curl_exec($ch); $code = (int)curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE); curl_close($ch);
        return ($b !== false && $code >= 200 && $code < 300) ? $b : null;
    }
    return null;
}
function stijl_schoon($k, $v) {
    if (!preg_match('/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{1,30}$/', (string)$k) || !is_array($v)) return null;
    $naam = trim((string)($v['naam'] ?? ''));
    if ($naam === '') return null;
    $fonts = (string)($v['fonts'] ?? '');
    if (!preg_match('/^[A-Za-z0-9+:;,@.&=_ -]{0,400}$/', $fonts)) $fonts = '';
    $css = str_replace(['<', '>'], '', (string)($v['css'] ?? ''));
    if (strlen($css) > 60000) $css = substr($css, 0, 60000);
    return ['naam' => cut($naam, 40), 'tekst' => cut(trim((string)($v['tekst'] ?? '')), 200), 'fonts' => $fonts, 'css' => $css, 'grote_foto' => !empty($v['grote_foto'])];
}
function stijlen_server() {
    static $lijst = null;
    if ($lijst !== null) return $lijst;
    $c = read_json(STIJLEN_CACHE, []);
    $nu = time();
    $vers = isset($c['stijlen']) && $nu - (int)($c['checked'] ?? 0) < 12 * 3600;
    $pas = $nu - (int)($c['tried'] ?? 0) < 900;
    if (!$vers && !$pas) {
        $server = function_exists('licentie_server') ? licentie_server() : 'https://formforge-sync-1.onrender.com';
        $body = stijl_http_get($server . '/api/website/stijlen');
        $d = $body !== null ? json_decode($body, true) : null;
        if (is_array($d) && isset($d['stijlen']) && is_array($d['stijlen'])) {
            $schoon = [];
            foreach ($d['stijlen'] as $k => $v) { $x = stijl_schoon($k, $v); if ($x) $schoon[$k] = $x; }
            $c = ['stijlen' => $schoon, 'checked' => $nu];
        }
        $c['tried'] = $nu;
        if (is_dir(DATA_DIR)) { @file_put_contents(STIJLEN_CACHE, json_encode($c, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES), LOCK_EX); }
    }
    $lijst = [];
    foreach ((array)($c['stijlen'] ?? []) as $k => $v) { $x = stijl_schoon($k, $v); if ($x) $lijst[$k] = $x; }
    return $lijst;
}
/* Alle stijlen: eerst de ingebouwde, aangevuld en eventueel verbeterd door de server */
function alle_stijlen() {
    global $STIJLEN;
    $uit = [];
    foreach ($STIJLEN as $k => $v) {
        $ib = stijl_css_ingebouwd($k);
        $uit[$k] = ['naam' => $v['naam'], 'tekst' => $v['tekst'], 'fonts' => $ib['fonts'], 'css' => $ib['css'], 'grote_foto' => $k === 'etalage'];
    }
    foreach (stijlen_server() as $k => $v) { $uit[$k] = $v; }
    return $uit;
}
function stijl() {
    $alle = alle_stijlen();
    $s = st('stijl');
    return isset($alle[$s]) ? $s : 'stoer';
}
function stijl_css($s) {
    $alle = alle_stijlen();
    if (!isset($alle[$s]) || trim($alle[$s]['css']) === '') return '';
    $link = $alle[$s]['fonts'] !== '' ? '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?' . h($alle[$s]['fonts']) . '&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />' . "\n" : '';
    return $link . "<style>\n/* stijl: " . $s . " */\n" . $alle[$s]['css'] . "\n</style>\n";
}
function stijl_grote_foto() {
    $alle = alle_stijlen();
    $s = stijl();
    return !empty($alle[$s]['grote_foto']);
}

function opbouw($deel) {
    global $OPBOUW;
    $v = st('opbouw_' . $deel);
    return isset($OPBOUW[$deel][$v]) ? $v : 'standaard';
}
function body_klassen($s) {
    return 't-' . $s . ' l-hero-' . opbouw('hero') . ' l-dienst-' . opbouw('diensten') . ' l-proj-' . opbouw('projecten')
         . ' l-menu-' . opbouw('menu') . ' l-strook-' . opbouw('strook') . ' l-afsl-' . opbouw('afsluiter') . ' l-voet-' . opbouw('voet');
}
function maatwerk_css() {
    global $LETTERS;
    $css = '';
    // Feeststijl met eigen kleuren: de eigen kleuren nog een keer, na de stijl
    if (st('feest_kleuren') === '0') { $css .= theme_css(); }
    $bg = valid_color(st('kleur_achtergrond'), '');
    $vl = valid_color(st('kleur_vlak'), '');
    $tx = valid_color(st('kleur_tekst'), '');
    if ($bg !== '') { $css .= "body,.section,.sec{background:{$bg};}\n"; }
    if ($vl !== '') { $css .= ":root{--sky:{$vl};} .section.sky,.sec.sky,.cwrap{background:{$vl};}\n"; }
    if ($tx !== '') { $css .= "body,.section,.sec,.card,.service{color:{$tx};}\n"; }
    $l = $LETTERS[st('lettertype')] ?? null;
    $link = '';
    if ($l && $l['fonts'] !== '') {
        $link = '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?' . h($l['fonts']) . '&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />' . "\n";
        $css .= "body{font-family:{$l['tekst']};} h1,h2,h3{font-family:{$l['kop']};}\n";
    }
    switch (st('vorm')) {
        case 'hoekig':
            $css .= ".btn,.menu a,.menu a.cta,.chatfab,.filters a{border-radius:3px !important;} .service,.card,.card .img,.cform,.cside,.others a,.gallery .ph,.photo-sheet,.photo-sheet img,.empty{border-radius:3px !important;} .folder .tab{border-radius:3px 3px 0 0 !important;} .folder .body{border-radius:0 4px 4px 4px !important;} .service .num{border-radius:3px !important;}\n";
            break;
        case 'afgerond':
            $css .= ".btn,.menu a,.menu a.cta,.filters a{border-radius:10px !important;} .service,.card,.cform,.cside,.empty{border-radius:14px !important;} .service .num{border-radius:12px !important;}\n";
            break;
        case 'rond':
            $css .= ".btn,.menu a,.menu a.cta,.chatfab,.filters a,.others a{border-radius:999px !important;} .service,.card,.cform,.cside,.empty{border-radius:28px !important;} .card .img{border-radius:28px 28px 0 0;} .gallery .ph,.photo-sheet,.photo-sheet img{border-radius:20px !important;} .folder .tab{border-radius:24px 24px 0 0 !important;} .folder .body{border-radius:0 30px 30px 30px !important;} .service .num{border-radius:50% !important;}\n";
            break;
    }
    $css .= <<<'CSS'

/* Grote foto bovenaan: bij de stijl Etalage, of als de klant dat kiest */
function hero_met_foto() {
    return stijl_grote_foto() || opbouw('hero') === 'foto';
}
/* Zet de gekozen stijl in een gemaakte pagina */
function stijl_pagina($html) {
    $s = stijl();
    $css = stijl_css($s);
    $kiezer = file_exists(DATA_DIR . '/demo-aan.txt');
    if ($kiezer) {
        // Voorbeeldsite: de stijl staat in een eigen blok, zodat de stijlkiezer hem kan wisselen
        $alle = alle_stijlen();
        $f = $alle[$s]['fonts'] ?? '';
        $css = '<link id="ff-fonts" rel="stylesheet" href="' . ($f !== '' ? 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?' . h($f) . '&display=swap' : 'data:text/css,') . '" />' . "\n"
             . '<style id="ff-stijl">' . "\n" . ($alle[$s]['css'] ?? '') . "\n</style>\n";
    }
    $css .= maatwerk_css();
    $pos = strpos($html, '</head>');
    if ($pos !== false) { $html = substr($html, 0, $pos) . $css . substr($html, $pos); }
    $pos = strpos($html, '<body>');
    if ($pos !== false) { $html = substr($html, 0, $pos) . '<body class="' . body_klassen($s) . '">' . ($kiezer ? stijl_kiezer($s) : '') . substr($html, $pos + 6); }
    return $html;
}

/* Eerste projectfoto, voor de grote foto in de stijl Etalage */
function hero_foto($pages) {
    list($featured, $projects) = site_lists($pages);
    foreach (array_merge($featured, $projects) as $p) { if (!empty($p['photo'])) return $p['photo']; }
    return '';
}
function paragraphs($text) {
    $out = [];
    foreach (preg_split('/\R{2,}/', trim((string)$text)) as $p) { if (trim($p) !== '') $out[] = trim($p); }
    return $out;
}
function home_title() {
    if (st('seo_titel') !== '') return st('seo_titel');
    $t = st('naam');
    if (st('eyebrow') !== '') $t .= ' | ' . st('eyebrow');
    return $t;
}
function home_desc() {
    if (st('seo_omschrijving') !== '') return cut(st('seo_omschrijving'), 160);
    $names = array_values(cat_names());
    $d = st('naam') . (st('plaats') !== '' ? ' uit ' . st('plaats') : '') . '. ' . st('hero_tekst');
    if (!empty($names)) $d .= ' Diensten: ' . implode(', ', $names) . '.';
    return cut($d, 160);
}

/* ===========================================================
   Opslag
   =========================================================== */
function ensure_data_dir() {
    if (!is_dir(DATA_DIR)) { mkdir(DATA_DIR, 0755, true); }
    if (!file_exists(DATA_DIR . '/.htaccess')) { file_put_contents(DATA_DIR . '/.htaccess', "Require all denied\n"); }
}
function ensure_upload_dir() {
    if (!is_dir(UPLOAD_DIR)) { mkdir(UPLOAD_DIR, 0755, true); }
    if (!file_exists(UPLOAD_DIR . '/.htaccess')) {
        file_put_contents(UPLOAD_DIR . '/.htaccess', "<FilesMatch \"\\.(php|php\\d|phtml|phar|pl|py|cgi|sh|htaccess)$\">\nRequire all denied\n</FilesMatch>\nOptions -Indexes\n");
    }
}
function read_json($f, $default) {
    if (!file_exists($f)) return $default;
    $d = json_decode(file_get_contents($f), true);
    return is_array($d) ? $d : $default;
}
function write_json($f, $data) {
    ensure_data_dir();
    file_put_contents($f, json_encode($data, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES), LOCK_EX);
}
function load_config() { return read_json(CONFIG_FILE, null); }
function save_config($c) { write_json(CONFIG_FILE, $c); }
function load_pages() { return read_json(PAGES_FILE, []); }
function save_pages($pages) { write_json(PAGES_FILE, array_values($pages)); }
function load_aanvragen() { return read_json(DATA_DIR . '/aanvragen.json', []); }

/* ===========================================================
   Hulpfuncties
   =========================================================== */
function h($s) { return htmlspecialchars((string)$s, ENT_QUOTES | ENT_SUBSTITUTE, 'UTF-8'); }
function cut($s, $n) { return function_exists('mb_substr') ? mb_substr($s, 0, $n) : substr($s, 0, $n); }

function slugify($text) {
    $text = trim($text);
    $from = ["\u{e1}","\u{e0}","\u{e4}","\u{e2}","\u{e9}","\u{e8}","\u{eb}","\u{ea}","\u{ed}","\u{ec}","\u{ef}","\u{ee}","\u{f3}","\u{f2}","\u{f6}","\u{f4}","\u{fa}","\u{f9}","\u{fc}","\u{fb}","\u{f1}","\u{e7}",
             "\u{c1}","\u{c0}","\u{c4}","\u{c2}","\u{c9}","\u{c8}","\u{cb}","\u{ca}","\u{cd}","\u{cc}","\u{cf}","\u{ce}","\u{d3}","\u{d2}","\u{d6}","\u{d4}","\u{da}","\u{d9}","\u{dc}","\u{db}","\u{d1}","\u{c7}"];
    $to   = ['a','a','a','a','e','e','e','e','i','i','i','i','o','o','o','o','u','u','u','u','n','c',
             'a','a','a','a','e','e','e','e','i','i','i','i','o','o','o','o','u','u','u','u','n','c'];
    $text = str_replace($from, $to, $text);
    $text = strtolower($text);
    $text = preg_replace('/[^a-z0-9]+/', '-', $text);
    $text = trim($text, '-');
    return $text === '' ? 'pagina' : $text;
}
/* Mappen van de site die de admin zelf maakt (dienstpagina's) */
function generated_slugs() { return read_json(GENERATED_FILE, []); }
function service_slugs() { return array_values(array_map(function($d) { return $d['slug']; }, services())); }

function unique_slug($base, $pages, $ignore = null) {
    global $RESERVED;
    $svc = service_slugs();
    $slug = $base; $i = 2;
    $taken = function($s) use ($pages, $ignore, $RESERVED, $svc) {
        if (in_array($s, $RESERVED, true) || in_array($s, $svc, true)) return true;
        foreach ($pages as $p) { if ($p['slug'] === $s && $p['slug'] !== $ignore) return true; }
        if ($s !== $ignore && file_exists(ROOT . '/' . $s)) return true;
        return false;
    };
    while ($taken($slug)) { $slug = $base . '-' . $i; $i++; }
    return $slug;
}
function rrmdir($dir) {
    if (!is_dir($dir)) return;
    foreach (scandir($dir) as $item) {
        if ($item === '.' || $item === '..') continue;
        $path = $dir . '/' . $item;
        is_dir($path) ? rrmdir($path) : @unlink($path);
    }
    @rmdir($dir);
}
function page_folder($p) {
    $f = $p['folder'] ?? '';
    return in_array($f, ['featured', 'projects'], true) ? $f : '';
}
function page_cats($p) {
    $names = cat_names();
    $out = [];
    foreach (($p['cats'] ?? []) as $c) { if (isset($names[$c]) && !in_array($c, $out, true)) { $out[] = $c; } }
    return $out;
}
function clean_cats($in) { return page_cats(['cats' => is_array($in) ? $in : []]); }
function photo_url($slug, $file) { return '/uploads/' . rawurlencode($slug) . '/' . rawurlencode($file); }
function page_title($p) { return trim($p['display_title'] ?? '') !== '' ? $p['display_title'] : $p['title']; }

/* Grote telefoonfoto's verkleinen en rechtop zetten (alleen jpg, alleen als de server dat kan) */
function shrink_photo($path) {
    $max = 2000;
    if (!function_exists('imagecreatefromjpeg') || !function_exists('exif_read_data')) return;
    $info = @getimagesize($path);
    if (!$info || $info[2] !== IMAGETYPE_JPEG) return;
    $w = $info[0]; $hgt = $info[1];
    $exif = @exif_read_data($path);
    $orient = is_array($exif) && isset($exif['Orientation']) ? (int)$exif['Orientation'] : 1;
    if (max($w, $hgt) <= $max && $orient === 1) return;
    $src = @imagecreatefromjpeg($path);
    if (!$src) return;
    if ($orient === 3) { $src = imagerotate($src, 180, 0); }
    elseif ($orient === 6) { $src = imagerotate($src, -90, 0); }
    elseif ($orient === 8) { $src = imagerotate($src, 90, 0); }
    $w = imagesx($src); $hgt = imagesy($src);
    $scale = min(1, $max / max($w, $hgt));
    $nw = (int)round($w * $scale); $nh = (int)round($hgt * $scale);
    $dst = imagecreatetruecolor($nw, $nh);
    imagecopyresampled($dst, $src, 0, 0, 0, 0, $nw, $nh, $w, $hgt);
    @imagejpeg($dst, $path, 84);
    imagedestroy($src); imagedestroy($dst);
}

/* Een geuploade afbeelding controleren en opslaan. Geeft bestandsnaam of '' terug. */
function save_one_image($tmp, $name, $dir, $prefix, &$errors) {
    $types = [IMAGETYPE_JPEG => 'jpg', IMAGETYPE_PNG => 'png', IMAGETYPE_WEBP => 'webp', IMAGETYPE_GIF => 'gif'];
    if (!is_uploaded_file($tmp)) return '';
    if (filesize($tmp) > MAX_PHOTO_BYTES) { $errors[] = "'" . $name . "' is groter dan 8 MB."; return ''; }
    $info = @getimagesize($tmp);
    if (!$info || !isset($types[$info[2]])) { $errors[] = "'" . $name . "' is geen jpg, png, webp of gif."; return ''; }
    if (!is_dir($dir)) { mkdir($dir, 0755, true); }
    $file = $prefix . date('Ymd-His') . '-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(4)) . '.' . $types[$info[2]];
    if (move_uploaded_file($tmp, $dir . '/' . $file)) { shrink_photo($dir . '/' . $file); return $file; }
    return '';
}
function upload_error_text($err, $name) {
    if ($err === UPLOAD_ERR_INI_SIZE || $err === UPLOAD_ERR_FORM_SIZE) return "'" . $name . "' is te groot voor de server.";
    return "'" . $name . "' kon niet worden geupload (foutcode " . $err . ").";
}
function save_uploaded_photos($slug, &$errors) {
    $saved = [];
    if (empty($_FILES['photos']) || !is_array($_FILES['photos']['name'])) return $saved;
    ensure_upload_dir();
    $count = count($_FILES['photos']['name']);
    for ($i = 0; $i < $count; $i++) {
        $err = $_FILES['photos']['error'][$i];
        if ($err === UPLOAD_ERR_NO_FILE) continue;
        if ($err !== UPLOAD_ERR_OK) { $errors[] = upload_error_text($err, $_FILES['photos']['name'][$i]); continue; }
        $f = save_one_image($_FILES['photos']['tmp_name'][$i], $_FILES['photos']['name'][$i], UPLOAD_DIR . '/' . $slug, '', $errors);
        if ($f !== '') $saved[] = $f;
    }
    return $saved;
}

/* ===========================================================
   SEO-onderdelen
   =========================================================== */
function business_jsonld() {
    $data = [
        '@context' => 'https://schema.org',
        '@type'    => 'LocalBusiness',
        '@id'      => site_url() . '/#bedrijf',
        'name'     => st('naam'),
        'url'      => site_url() . '/',
    ];
    if (logo_url() !== '') { $data['logo'] = site_url() . logo_url(); $data['image'] = site_url() . logo_url(); }
    if (st('tel') !== '') $data['telephone'] = tel_link(st('tel'));
    if (st('email') !== '') $data['email'] = st('email');
    if (st('straat') !== '' || st('plaats') !== '') {
        $data['address'] = array_filter([
            '@type'           => 'PostalAddress',
            'streetAddress'   => st('straat'),
            'postalCode'      => st('postcode'),
            'addressLocality' => st('plaats'),
            'addressRegion'   => st('provincie'),
            'addressCountry'  => 'NL',
        ], function($v) { return $v !== ''; });
    }
    if (st('werkgebied') !== '') $data['areaServed'] = st('werkgebied');
    $names = array_values(cat_names());
    if (!empty($names)) $data['knowsAbout'] = $names;
    return '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_HEX_TAG) . '</script>';
}

function meta_desc_for($p) {
    $d = trim($p['desc'] ?? '');
    if ($d === '') { $d = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $p['text'] ?? '')); }
    $names = cat_names();
    $cats = array_map(function($c) use ($names) { return $names[$c]; }, page_cats($p));
    $base = page_title($p) . (empty($cats) ? '' : ' (' . implode(', ', $cats) . ')') . '. Een project van ' . st('naam') . (st('plaats') !== '' ? ' uit ' . st('plaats') : '') . '.';
    $d = $d === '' ? $base : $d . ' - ' . $base;
    return cut($d, 155);
}

/* ===========================================================
   Vaste delen van elke pagina
   =========================================================== */
function theme_css() {
    $d = valid_color(st('kleur_donker'), '#0b1f3a');
    $a = valid_color(st('kleur_accent'), '#1d4ed8');
    return ":root{--navy:{$d};--navy-2:{$d};--blue:{$a};--stripe:{$a};--sky:#eef2f8;--line:#d7e0ee;--muted:#51607a;}\n"
         . "@supports (color: color-mix(in srgb, red 50%, blue)){:root{--navy-2:color-mix(in srgb,{$d} 86%,#fff);--stripe:color-mix(in srgb,{$a} 55%,#fff);--sky:color-mix(in srgb,{$a} 7%,#fff);--line:color-mix(in srgb,{$a} 14%,#e2e7ef);}}\n";
}

function brand_html() {
    $logo = logo_url();
    $img = $logo !== '' ? '<img src="' . h($logo) . '" alt="" />' : '';
    return '<a class="brand" href="/" aria-label="' . h(st('naam')) . ', naar de voorpagina">' . $img . '<span class="name">' . h(st('naam')) . '</span></a>';
}
function slogan_bar() {
    return st('slogan') !== '' ? '<div class="notice"><div class="wrap">' . h(st('slogan')) . '</div></div>' . "\n" : '';
}
/* $backUrl leeg = menu van de voorpagina */
function site_header($backUrl = '', $backLabel = '') {
    $out = slogan_bar() . '<header class="top"><div class="wrap">' . brand_html() . '<nav class="menu" aria-label="Menu">';
    if ($backUrl === '') {
        if (!empty(services())) { $out .= '<a class="nav" href="/#diensten">Diensten</a>'; }
        $out .= '<a class="nav" href="/#projecten">Projecten</a>';
    } else {
        $out .= '<a class="back" href="' . h($backUrl) . '" onclick="try{var r=document.referrer;if(r&&r.indexOf(location.origin+\'/\')===0&&history.length>1){history.back();return false;}}catch(e){}return true;"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M19 12H5M11 18l-6-6 6-6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg><span class="long">' . h($backLabel) . '</span><span class="short">Terug</span></a>';
    }
    $out .= '<a class="cta" href="/contact/">Contact</a></nav></div></header>' . "\n";
    return $out;
}
function nap_block() {
    $cols = [];
    $adres = array_filter([st('straat'), trim(st('postcode') . ' ' . st('plaats'))]);
    $c = '<strong>' . h(st('naam')) . '</strong>';
    foreach ($adres as $r) { $c .= '<br />' . h($r); }
    if (st('kvk') !== '') { $c .= '<br />KvK ' . h(st('kvk')); }
    $cols[] = $c;
    $contact = [];
    if (st('tel') !== '') $contact[] = '<a href="tel:' . h(tel_link(st('tel'))) . '">' . h(st('tel')) . '</a>';
    if (st('tel2') !== '') $contact[] = '<a href="tel:' . h(tel_link(st('tel2'))) . '">' . h(st('tel2')) . '</a>';
    if (st('email') !== '') $contact[] = '<a href="mailto:' . h(st('email')) . '">' . h(st('email')) . '</a>';
    $contact[] = '<a href="/contact/">Contactformulier</a>';
    $cols[] = '<strong>Contact</strong><br />' . implode('<br />', $contact);
    $extra = '';
    if (st('tijden') !== '') $extra .= '<strong>Openingstijden</strong><br />' . nl2br(h(st('tijden')));
    if (st('werkgebied') !== '') $extra .= ($extra !== '' ? '<br />' : '') . '<strong>Werkgebied</strong><br />' . h(st('werkgebied'));
    if ($extra !== '') $cols[] = $extra;
    $links = [];
    foreach (services() as $k => $s) { $links[] = '<a href="' . h(service_url($k)) . '">' . h($s['naam']) . '</a>'; }
    if (!empty($links)) $cols[] = '<strong>Diensten</strong><br />' . implode('<br />', $links);
    $out = '<div class="nap">';
    foreach ($cols as $c) { $out .= '<div>' . $c . '</div>'; }
    return $out . '</div>';
}
function chat_fab() {
    $c = chat_url();
    if ($c === '') return '';
    return '<a class="chatfab" href="' . h($c) . '" aria-label="Chat met ' . h(st('naam')) . '"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 5.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 6.5 3h11A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 20 5.5v8a2.5 2.5 0 0 1-2.5 2.5H10l-4.5 4v-4h0A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 4 14.5z" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="M8 8.5h8M8 11.5h5" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg><span>Chat met ons</span></a>' . "\n";
}
function site_footer() {
    $y = date('Y');
    return '<footer class="foot"><div class="wrap">' . nap_block()
         . '<div class="legal"><span>&copy; ' . $y . ' ' . h(st('naam')) . '</span><span class="logins"><a class="login" href="/admin.php" rel="nofollow">Website beheren</a><a class="login" href="/admin.php?adm" rel="nofollow">Administratie</a></span></div>'
         . '</div></footer>' . "\n" . chat_fab();
}
/* Blauwe balk onderaan met contactknoppen */
function cta_band($dienstKey = '') {
    $q = $dienstKey !== '' ? '?dienst=' . rawurlencode($dienstKey) : '';
    $out = '<section class="band"><div class="wrap"><div><h2>' . h(st('cta_titel')) . '</h2><p>' . h(st('cta_tekst')) . '</p></div><div class="acts">';
    if (chat_url() !== '') { $out .= '<a class="btn white" href="' . h(chat_url()) . '">Chat direct met ons</a>'; }
    elseif (st('tel') !== '') { $out .= '<a class="btn white" href="tel:' . h(tel_link(st('tel'))) . '">Bel ' . h(st('tel')) . '</a>'; }
    $out .= '<a class="btn outline" href="/contact/' . h($q) . '">Contact opnemen</a></div></div></section>' . "\n";
    return $out;
}

/* ===========================================================
   Voorpagina (index.html)
   =========================================================== */
function project_item($p) {
    $photos = $p['photos'] ?? [];
    return [
        'title' => page_title($p),
        'url'   => '/' . $p['slug'] . '/',
        'desc'  => trim($p['desc'] ?? ''),
        'photo' => !empty($photos) ? photo_url($p['slug'], $photos[0]) : '',
        'date'  => $p['updated'] ?? ($p['created'] ?? ''),
        'cats'  => page_cats($p),
    ];
}
function site_lists($pages) {
    $featured = []; $projects = [];
    foreach ($pages as $p) {
        $folder = page_folder($p);
        if ($folder === '') continue;
        $item = project_item($p);
        $projects[] = $item;
        if ($folder === 'featured') { $featured[] = $item; }
    }
    $sort = function($a, $b) { return strcmp($b['date'], $a['date']); };
    usort($projects, $sort); usort($featured, $sort);
    return [$featured, $projects];
}

/* ===========================================================
   Blokken op de voorpagina: volgorde en aan/uit, plus de nieuwe blokken
   =========================================================== */
$BLOKKEN = ['strook' => 'Balk met diensten', 'diensten' => 'Diensten', 'projecten' => 'Uitgelichte projecten', 'voorna' => 'Voor en na (foto\'s)', 'werkwijze' => 'Werkwijze in stappen', 'cijfers' => 'Cijfers', 'over' => 'Over ons', 'vragen' => 'Veelgestelde vragen'];
function blokken_volgorde() {
    global $ST, $BLOKKEN;
    $uit = []; $gezien = [];
    foreach ((array)($ST['blokken'] ?? []) as $b) {
        $k = (string)($b['k'] ?? '');
        if (!isset($BLOKKEN[$k]) || isset($gezien[$k])) continue;
        $gezien[$k] = 1; $uit[] = ['k' => $k, 'aan' => !empty($b['aan'])];
    }
    foreach (array_keys($BLOKKEN) as $k) { if (!isset($gezien[$k])) { $uit[] = ['k' => $k, 'aan' => in_array($k, ['strook', 'diensten', 'projecten'], true)]; } }
    return $uit;
}
function blok_lijst($k) { global $ST; return is_array($ST[$k] ?? null) ? $ST[$k] : []; }
function blok_inhoud($k) {
    if ($k === 'voorna') { return array_values(array_filter(blok_lijst('voorna'), function($x) { return ($x['voor'] ?? '') !== '' && ($x['na'] ?? '') !== ''; })); }
    if ($k === 'werkwijze') { return array_values(array_filter(blok_lijst('werkwijze'), function($x) { return trim($x['titel'] ?? '') !== ''; })); }
    if ($k === 'cijfers') { return array_values(array_filter(blok_lijst('cijfers'), function($x) { return trim($x['getal'] ?? '') !== ''; })); }
    if ($k === 'vragen') { return array_values(array_filter(blok_lijst('vragen'), function($x) { return trim($x['vraag'] ?? '') !== '' && trim($x['antwoord'] ?? '') !== ''; })); }
    if ($k === 'over') { return st('over_tekst') !== '' ? [1] : []; }
    return [];
}
function blok_kop($titel, $intro) {
    $o = '<div class="head"><div><h2>' . h($titel) . '</h2>';
    if ($intro !== '') { $o .= '<p>' . h($intro) . '</p>'; }
    return $o . '</div></div>';
}
function blok_html($k, $sky) {
    $items = blok_inhoud($k);
    if (!$items) return '';
    $cl = 'section blok-' . $k . ($sky ? ' sky' : '');
    $o = '';
    if ($k === 'voorna') {
        $o .= '<section class="' . $cl . '" id="voor-en-na"><div class="wrap">' . blok_kop(st('voorna_titel') ?: 'Voor en na', st('voorna_intro')) . '<div class="vn-lijst' . (count($items) === 1 ? ' een' : '') . '">';
        foreach ($items as $x) {
            $o .= '<figure class="vn-item"><div class="vn" style="--p:50%"><img src="/uploads/site/' . h(rawurlencode($x['na'])) . '" alt="Na' . (($x['tekst'] ?? '') !== '' ? ': ' . h($x['tekst']) : '') . '" loading="lazy" />'
                . '<div class="vn-voor"><img src="/uploads/site/' . h(rawurlencode($x['voor'])) . '" alt="Voor' . (($x['tekst'] ?? '') !== '' ? ': ' . h($x['tekst']) : '') . '" loading="lazy" /></div>'
                . '<span class="vn-lijn" aria-hidden="true"><span></span></span><span class="vn-l vn-l1">Voor</span><span class="vn-l vn-l2">Na</span>'
                . '<input class="vn-schuif" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="50" aria-label="Schuif tussen voor en na" /></div>';
            if (($x['tekst'] ?? '') !== '') { $o .= '<figcaption>' . h($x['tekst']) . '</figcaption>'; }
            $o .= '</figure>';
        }
        $o .= '</div></div></section>' . "\n";
    } elseif ($k === 'werkwijze') {
        $o .= '<section class="' . $cl . '" id="werkwijze"><div class="wrap">' . blok_kop(st('werkwijze_titel') ?: 'Zo werken wij', st('werkwijze_intro')) . '<ol class="ww-stappen">';
        foreach ($items as $i => $x) { $o .= '<li class="ww-stap"><span class="ww-nr">' . ($i + 1) . '</span><h3>' . h($x['titel']) . '</h3>' . (($x['tekst'] ?? '') !== '' ? '<p>' . h($x['tekst']) . '</p>' : '') . '</li>'; }
        $o .= '</ol></div></section>' . "\n";
    } elseif ($k === 'cijfers') {
        $o .= '<section class="' . $cl . '" id="cijfers"><div class="wrap">' . (st('cijfers_titel') !== '' ? blok_kop(st('cijfers_titel'), '') : '') . '<div class="cf-rij">';
        foreach ($items as $x) { $o .= '<div class="cf"><strong>' . h($x['getal']) . '</strong><span>' . h($x['label'] ?? '') . '</span></div>'; }
        $o .= '</div></div></section>' . "\n";
    } elseif ($k === 'over') {
        $foto = st('over_foto');
        $o .= '<section class="' . $cl . '" id="over-ons"><div class="wrap"><div class="ov' . ($foto === '' ? ' zonder' : '') . '">';
        if ($foto !== '') { $o .= '<div class="ov-foto"><img src="/uploads/site/' . h(rawurlencode($foto)) . '" alt="' . h(st('over_naam') !== '' ? st('over_naam') : st('naam')) . '" loading="lazy" /></div>'; }
        $o .= '<div class="ov-tekst"><h2>' . h(st('over_titel') ?: 'Over ons') . '</h2>';
        foreach (preg_split("/\n\s*\n/", st('over_tekst')) as $al) { $o .= '<p>' . nl2br(h(trim($al))) . '</p>'; }
        if (st('over_naam') !== '') { $o .= '<p class="ov-naam">' . h(st('over_naam')) . '</p>'; }
        $o .= '</div></div></div></section>' . "\n";
    } elseif ($k === 'vragen') {
        $o .= '<section class="' . $cl . '" id="vragen"><div class="wrap">' . blok_kop(st('vragen_titel') ?: 'Veelgestelde vragen', '') . '<div class="fq">';
        foreach ($items as $x) { $o .= '<details class="fq-item"><summary>' . h($x['vraag']) . '</summary><div class="fq-a">' . nl2br(h($x['antwoord'])) . '</div></details>'; }
        $o .= '</div></div></section>' . "\n";
    }
    return $o;
}
function blokken_jsonld() {
    foreach (blokken_volgorde() as $b) {
        if ($b['k'] !== 'vragen' || !$b['aan']) continue;
        $v = blok_inhoud('vragen');
        if (!$v) return '';
        $ld = ['@context' => 'https://schema.org', '@type' => 'FAQPage', 'mainEntity' => array_map(function($x) { return ['@type' => 'Question', 'name' => $x['vraag'], 'acceptedAnswer' => ['@type' => 'Answer', 'text' => $x['antwoord']]]; }, $v)];
        return "\n" . '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($ld, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_HEX_TAG) . '</script>';
    }
    return '';
}

    $cfg = [
        'services' => $svc, 'featured' => $featured, 'projects' => $projects,
        'title' => home_title(), 'naam' => st('naam'),
    ];
    $json = json_encode($cfg, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_HEX_TAG | JSON_HEX_AMP | JSON_HEX_APOS | JSON_HEX_QUOT);

    $out .= site_header();

    $logo = logo_url();
    $foto = hero_met_foto() ? hero_foto($pages) : '';
    $heroAttr = $foto !== '' ? ' class="hero has-photo" style="background-image:url(\'' . h($foto) . '\')"' : ' class="hero"';
    $out .= '<main id="home" class="view"><section' . $heroAttr . '><span class="hero-rand" aria-hidden="true"></span><div class="wrap' . ($logo === '' ? ' solo' : '') . '"><div>';

    $out .= '<h1>' . h(st('hero_titel')) . '</h1>';
    if (st('hero_tekst') !== '') $out .= '<p class="lead">' . h(st('hero_tekst')) . '</p>';
    $out .= '<div class="actions"><a class="btn primary" href="/contact/">' . h(st('knop_tekst') !== '' ? st('knop_tekst') : 'Neem contact op') . '</a>';
    if (!empty(services())) $out .= '<a class="btn quiet" href="#diensten">' . h(st('diensten_titel')) . '</a>';
    $out .= '</div></div>';
    if ($logo !== '') $out .= '<div class="emblem"><img src="' . h($logo) . '" alt="Logo ' . h(st('naam')) . '" /></div>';
    $out .= '</div></section>' . "\n";

    $sky = false;
    foreach (blokken_volgorde() as $blok) {
        if (!$blok['aan']) continue;
        $k = $blok['k'];
        if ($k === 'strook') {
            if (empty(services())) continue;
            $out .= '<div class="servicebar" aria-hidden="true"><div class="wrap">';
            foreach (services() as $s) { $out .= '<span>' . h($s['naam']) . '</span>'; }
            for ($kopie = 0; $kopie < 3; $kopie++) { foreach (services() as $s) { $out .= '<span class="ff-dup">' . h($s['naam']) . '</span>'; } }
            $out .= '</div></div>' . "\n";
        } elseif ($k === 'diensten') {
            if (empty(services())) continue;
            $out .= '<section class="section' . ($sky ? ' sky' : '') . '" id="diensten"><div class="wrap"><div class="head"><div><h2>' . h(st('diensten_titel')) . '</h2>';
            if (st('diensten_intro') !== '') $out .= '<p>' . h(st('diensten_intro')) . '</p>';
            $out .= '</div></div><div class="services" id="services"></div></div></section>' . "\n";
            $sky = !$sky;
        } elseif ($k === 'projecten') {
            if (empty($featured)) continue;
            $out .= '<section class="section' . ($sky ? ' sky' : '') . '" id="featuredSection" hidden><div class="wrap"><div class="head"><div><h2>Uitgelichte projecten</h2><p>Een greep uit ons werk.</p></div><a class="textlink" href="#projecten">Alle projecten bekijken</a></div><div class="folders" id="featured"></div></div></section>' . "\n";
            $sky = !$sky;
        } else {
            $b = blok_html($k, $sky);
            if ($b !== '') { $out .= $b; $sky = !$sky; }
        }
    }
    $out .= cta_band() . '</main>' . "\n";

    $out .= '<main id="projects" class="view" hidden><section class="crumb"><div class="wrap">';
    $out .= '<a class="backlink" href="/" data-go="home"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M19 12H5M11 18l-6-6 6-6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>Terug naar home</a>';
    $out .= '<h1><span id="projTitle">Projecten</span> <span class="count" id="projCount">0</span></h1><p id="projLead"></p><nav class="filters" id="filters" aria-label="Filter op dienst"></nav></div></section>';
    $out .= '<section class="section sky"><div class="wrap"><div class="cards" id="cards"></div></div></section></main>' . "\n";

    $out .= site_footer();
    $js = <<<'JS'
<script>
(function () {
  var CFG = __DATA__;
  var SERVICES = CFG.services || [];
  var data = { featured: CFG.featured || [], projects: CFG.projects || [] };
  var arrow = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c];
    });
  }
  function serviceByKey(k) {
    for (var i = 0; i < SERVICES.length; i++) { if (SERVICES[i].key === k) { return SERVICES[i]; } }
    return null;
  }
  function countFor(k) {
    return data.projects.filter(function (p) { return (p.cats || []).indexOf(k) !== -1; }).length;
  }

  function renderServices() {
    var box = document.getElementById("services");
    if (!box) { return; }
    box.innerHTML = SERVICES.map(function (s, i) {
      var n = countFor(s.key);
      return '<a class="service" href="' + esc(s.page) + '"><span class="num">' + ("0" + (i + 1)).slice(-2) + '</span>' +
        '<h3>' + esc(s.name) + '</h3><p>' + esc(s.text) + '</p>' +
        '<span class="go">Meer informatie' + (n ? ' <span class="n">(' + n + (n === 1 ? ' project' : ' projecten') + ')</span>' : '') + ' ' + arrow + '</span></a>';
    }).join("");
  }

  function renderFeatured() {
    var featured = data.featured.slice(0, 2);
    var sec = document.getElementById("featuredSection");
    var box = document.getElementById("featured");
    if (!sec || !box) { return; }
    if (!featured.length) { sec.hidden = true; return; }
    sec.hidden = false;
    box.className = "folders" + (featured.length === 1 ? " single" : "");
    box.innerHTML = featured.map(function (p, i) {
      var sheet = p.photo
        ? '<div class="photo-sheet" aria-hidden="true"><img src="' + esc(p.photo) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" /></div>'
        : '<div class="photo-sheet empty" aria-hidden="true">Nog geen foto</div>';
      return '<a class="folder ' + (i === 0 ? "a" : "b") + '" href="' + esc(p.url) + '">' +
        '<span class="tab" aria-hidden="true"></span><div class="body">' + sheet +
        '<div class="front"><h3>' + esc(p.title) + '</h3>' +
        (p.desc ? '<p class="desc">' + esc(p.desc) + '</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="meta"><span class="open">Bekijk project ' + arrow + '</span></div></div></div></a>';
    }).join("");
  }

  function renderProjects(cat) {
    var s = cat ? serviceByKey(cat) : null;
    if (cat && !s) { cat = ""; }
    var list = data.projects.filter(function (p) { return !cat || (p.cats || []).indexOf(cat) !== -1; });
    document.getElementById("projTitle").textContent = s ? s.name : "Projecten";
    document.getElementById("projLead").textContent = s ? s.text : "Een overzicht van ons werk. Kies een project voor de foto's en het verhaal.";
    document.getElementById("projCount").textContent = list.length;
    document.getElementById("filters").innerHTML = SERVICES.length ?
      '<a href="#projecten"' + (!cat ? ' class="on" aria-current="true"' : '') + '>Alle projecten</a>' +
      SERVICES.map(function (x) {
        return '<a href="#projecten/' + esc(x.key) + '"' + (x.key === cat ? ' class="on" aria-current="true"' : '') + '>' + esc(x.name) + '</a>';
      }).join("") : "";
    document.getElementById("cards").innerHTML = list.length ? list.map(function (p) {
      var tags = (p.cats || []).map(serviceByKey).filter(Boolean).map(function (x) { return '<span>' + esc(x.name) + '</span>'; }).join("");
      return '<a class="card" href="' + esc(p.url) + '"><div class="img">' +
        (p.photo ? '<img src="' + esc(p.photo) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" />' : '') +
        '</div><div class="txt">' + (tags ? '<div class="tags">' + tags + '</div>' : '') +
        '<h3>' + esc(p.title) + '</h3><p>' + esc(p.desc || "") +
        '</p><span class="go">Bekijk project ' + arrow + '</span></div></a>';
    }).join("") : '<p class="empty">' + (cat ? "Er staan nog geen projecten in deze categorie. Binnenkort meer." : "Er zijn nog geen projecten gepubliceerd.") + '</p>';
  }

  function route() {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || "");
    var onProjects = h === "#projecten" || h.indexOf("#projecten/") === 0;
    document.getElementById("home").hidden = onProjects;
    document.getElementById("projects").hidden = !onProjects;
    if (onProjects) {
      var cat = h.length > 11 ? h.slice(11) : "";
      renderProjects(cat);
      var s = serviceByKey(cat);
      document.title = (s ? s.name + " - " : "Projecten - ") + CFG.naam;
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    } else {
      document.title = CFG.title;
      var el = h.length > 1 ? document.getElementById(h.slice(1)) : null;
      if (el) { el.scrollIntoView(); } else { window.scrollTo(0, 0); }
    }
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-go="home"]').forEach(function (a) {
    a.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (location.hash) { history.pushState("", document.title, location.pathname + location.search); }
      route();
    });
  });
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
  window.addEventListener("popstate", route);

  document.querySelectorAll(".vn").forEach(function (v) {
    var r = v.querySelector(".vn-schuif");
    if (!r) { return; }
    var zet = function () { v.style.setProperty("--p", r.value + "%"); };
    r.addEventListener("input", zet); zet();
  });
  renderServices();
  renderFeatured();
  route();
})();
</script>
JS;
    $out .= str_replace('__DATA__', $json, $js) . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

/* ===========================================================
   Dienstpagina
   =========================================================== */
function render_service_page($key, $pages) {
    $all = services();
    $s = $all[$key];
    $name = $s['naam'];
    $intro = trim($s['kort'] ?? '');
    $titel = $name . (st('plaats') !== '' ? ' in ' . st('plaats') . (st('werkgebied') !== '' ? ' en omgeving' : '') : '');
    $meta = cut($name . ' door ' . st('naam') . (st('plaats') !== '' ? ' uit ' . st('plaats') : '') . '. ' . ($intro !== '' ? $intro . ' ' : '') . (st('werkgebied') !== '' ? 'Werkgebied: ' . st('werkgebied') . '.' : ''), 160);
    $svcLd = [
        '@context' => 'https://schema.org', '@type' => 'Service', 'name' => $name, 'serviceType' => $name,
        'description' => $meta, 'url' => site_url() . '/' . $s['slug'] . '/', 'provider' => ['@id' => site_url() . '/#bedrijf'],
    ];
    if (st('werkgebied') !== '') $svcLd['areaServed'] = st('werkgebied');
    $extra = business_jsonld() . "\n" . '<script type="application/ld+json">' . json_encode($svcLd, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_HEX_TAG) . '</script>';
    $out = site_head($titel . ' | ' . st('naam'), $meta, '/' . $s['slug'] . '/', $extra, true);
    $out .= <<<'CSS'
<style>
  .shero { background:var(--navy); color:#fff; padding:clamp(48px,7vw,96px) 0 clamp(44px,6vw,80px); }
  .shero .eyebrow { display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; font-size:13px; font-weight:600; letter-spacing:.14em; text-transform:uppercase; color:rgba(255,255,255,.7); margin-bottom:18px; }
  .shero .eyebrow::before { content:""; width:28px; height:3px; border-radius:3px; background:var(--stripe); }
  .shero h1 { font-size:clamp(34px,5.4vw,62px); max-width:20ch; }
  .shero p { margin:18px 0 0; color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); max-width:56ch; font-size:19px; }
  .shero .acts { margin-top:30px; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:12px; }
  .sbody { padding:clamp(48px,7vw,88px) 0; }
  .stext { max-width:68ch; font-size:17.5px; }
  .stext p { margin:0 0 1.1em; }
  .sec { padding:clamp(48px,7vw,88px) 0; }
  .sec.sky { background:var(--sky); }
  .sec h2 { font-size:clamp(28px,3.6vw,42px); line-height:1.1; margin:0 0 28px; }
  .others { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:10px; }
  .others a { text-decoration:none; font-weight:600; font-size:15px; padding:10px 16px; border-radius:999px; border:1.5px solid var(--line); background:#fff; }
  .others a:hover { border-color:var(--blue); color:var(--blue); }
</style>
</head>
<body>
CSS;
    $out .= site_header('/#diensten', 'Alle diensten');
    $out .= '<section class="shero"><div class="wrap">';
    if (st('eyebrow') !== '') $out .= '<div class="eyebrow">' . h(st('eyebrow')) . '</div>';
    $out .= '<h1>' . h($titel) . '</h1>';
    if ($intro !== '') $out .= '<p>' . h($intro) . '</p>';
    $out .= '<div class="acts"><a class="btn primary" href="/contact/?dienst=' . h(rawurlencode($key)) . '">Contact opnemen</a>';
    if (st('tel') !== '') $out .= '<a class="btn quiet" href="tel:' . h(tel_link(st('tel'))) . '">Bel ' . h(st('tel')) . '</a>';
    if (chat_url() !== '') $out .= '<a class="btn quiet" href="' . h(chat_url()) . '">Chat met ons</a>';
    $out .= '</div></div></section>';

    $out .= '<main>';
    $paras = paragraphs($s['tekst'] ?? '');
    if (!empty($paras)) {
        $out .= '<section class="sbody"><div class="wrap"><div class="stext">';
        foreach ($paras as $para) { $out .= '<p>' . nl2br(h($para)) . '</p>'; }
        $out .= '</div></div></section>';
    }

    $list = [];
    foreach ($pages as $p) {
        if (page_folder($p) === '') continue;
        if (!in_array($key, page_cats($p), true)) continue;
        $list[] = $p;
    }
    usort($list, function($a, $b) { return strcmp($b['updated'] ?? ($b['created'] ?? ''), $a['updated'] ?? ($a['created'] ?? '')); });
    $out .= '<section class="sec sky"><div class="wrap"><h2>Projecten: ' . h($name) . '</h2>';
    if (empty($list)) {
        $out .= '<p class="empty">Hier verschijnen binnenkort onze projecten.</p>';
    } else {
        $out .= '<div class="cards">';
        foreach ($list as $p) {
            $t = page_title($p);
            $photos = $p['photos'] ?? [];
            $img = !empty($photos) ? '<img src="' . h(photo_url($p['slug'], $photos[0])) . '" alt="' . h($t) . '" loading="lazy" />' : '';
            $out .= '<a class="card" href="/' . h($p['slug']) . '/"><div class="img">' . $img . '</div><div class="txt"><h3>' . h($t) . '</h3>';
            if (trim($p['desc'] ?? '') !== '') { $out .= '<p>' . h($p['desc']) . '</p>'; }
            $out .= '</div></a>';
        }
        $out .= '</div>';
    }
    $out .= '</div></section>';

    if (count($all) > 1) {
        $out .= '<section class="sec"><div class="wrap"><h2>Andere diensten</h2><div class="others">';
        foreach ($all as $k => $x) {
            if ($k === $key) continue;
            $out .= '<a href="' . h(service_url($k)) . '">' . h($x['naam']) . '</a>';
        }
        $out .= '</div></div></section>';
    }
    $out .= '</main>' . cta_band($key) . site_footer() . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

    $out .= site_header('/', 'Naar home');

    $out .= '</form>';

    $side = '';

    if (st('tel') !== '') $direct[] = '<a href="tel:' . h(tel_link(st('tel'))) . '">' . h(st('tel')) . '</a>';

    if (st('email') !== '') $direct[] = '<a href="mailto:' . h(st('email')) . '">' . h(st('email')) . '</a>';

    $adres = array_filter([st('straat'), trim(st('postcode') . ' ' . st('plaats'))]);
    if (!empty($adres)) $side .= '<h3>Adres</h3><p>' . h(st('naam')) . '<br />' . implode('<br />', array_map('h', $adres)) . '</p>';
    if (st('tijden') !== '') $side .= '<h3>Openingstijden</h3><p>' . nl2br(h(st('tijden'))) . '</p>';

    if ($side !== '') $out .= '<aside class="cside">' . $side . '</aside>';
    $out .= '</div></main>';
    $out .= <<<'JS'
<script>
(function () {
  var f = document.getElementById('formulier');
  document.getElementById('t').value = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  var sel = document.getElementById('dienst');
  var d = (q.get('dienst') || '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  if (sel && d && sel.querySelector('option[value="' + d + '"]')) { sel.value = d; }
  var meldingen = {
    naam: 'Vul uw naam in.',
    contact: 'Vul een telefoonnummer of e-mailadres in, zodat wij u kunnen bereiken.',
    email: 'Het e-mailadres lijkt niet te kloppen.',
    telefoon: 'Het telefoonnummer lijkt niet te kloppen.',
    bericht: 'Vertel kort waar wij u mee kunnen helpen.',
    tijd: 'Er ging iets mis. Probeer het nog een keer.',
    limiet: 'U heeft al meerdere berichten verstuurd. Probeer het later opnieuw of bel ons.'
  };
  var box = document.getElementById('fout');
  function toon(code) { box.textContent = meldingen[code] || meldingen.tijd; box.style.display = 'block'; }
  if (q.get('fout')) { toon(q.get('fout')); }
  f.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    var naam = f.naam.value.trim(), tel = f.telefoon.value.trim(), mail = f.email.value.trim(), ber = f.bericht.value.trim();
    var code = '';
    if (!naam) { code = 'naam'; }
    else if (!tel && !mail) { code = 'contact'; }
    else if (mail && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(mail)) { code = 'email'; }
    else if (tel && !/^[0-9 +()\-]{6,30}$/.test(tel)) { code = 'telefoon'; }
    else if (!ber) { code = 'bericht'; }
    if (code) { e.preventDefault(); toon(code); box.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' }); return; }
    document.getElementById('send').disabled = true;
  });
})();
</script>
JS;
    $out .= site_footer() . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

"\n</head>\n<body>\n";
    $out .= site_header('/', 'Naar home');

    $out .= '</p><div class="acts"><a class="btn primary" href="/">Terug naar de voorpagina</a><a class="btn quiet" href="/#projecten">Bekijk onze projecten</a></div></div></main>';
    $out .= site_footer() . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

/* ===========================================================
   Projectpagina's
   =========================================================== */
function render_project_page($p) {
    $title = page_title($p);
    $ph = $p['photos'] ?? [];
    $out = site_head($title, meta_desc_for($p), '/' . $p['slug'] . '/', '', false, !empty($ph) ? photo_url($p['slug'], $ph[0]) : '');
    $out .= <<<'CSS'
<style>
  .phero { background:var(--navy); color:#fff; padding:clamp(48px,7vw,88px) 0 clamp(40px,6vw,72px); }
  .phero h1 { font-size:clamp(34px,5.4vw,60px); max-width:20ch; }
  .phero p { margin:16px 0 0; color:rgba(255,255,255,.8); max-width:60ch; font-size:18px; }
  .ptags { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:8px; margin-bottom:18px; }
  .ptags a { font-size:13px; font-weight:600; text-decoration:none; color:#fff; background:rgba(255,255,255,.12); border-radius:999px; padding:4px 12px; }
  .ptags a:hover { background:rgba(255,255,255,.22); }
  .pbody { padding:clamp(40px,6vw,72px) 0; }
  .ptext { max-width:68ch; font-size:17px; }
  .ptext p { margin:0 0 1em; }
  .gallery { columns:3 300px; column-gap:18px; margin-top:32px; }
  .gallery.n2 { columns:2 300px; }
  .gallery.n1 { columns:1; max-width:820px; }
  .gallery .ph { break-inside:avoid; -webkit-column-break-inside:avoid; margin:0 0 18px; border-radius:12px; overflow:hidden; background:var(--sky); box-shadow:0 10px 28px -18px rgba(0,0,0,.45); }
  .gallery img { width:100%; height:auto; display:block; }
</style>
</head>
<body>
CSS;
    $out .= site_header('/#projecten', 'Terug naar projecten');
    $out .= '<section class="phero"><div class="wrap">';
    $cats = page_cats($p);
    $names = cat_names();
    if (!empty($cats)) {
        $out .= '<div class="ptags">';
        foreach ($cats as $c) { $out .= '<a href="' . h(service_url($c)) . '">' . h($names[$c]) . '</a>'; }
        $out .= '</div>';
    }
    $out .= '<h1>' . h($title) . '</h1>';
    if (trim($p['desc'] ?? '') !== '') { $out .= '<p>' . h($p['desc']) . '</p>'; }
    $out .= '</div></section><main class="pbody"><div class="wrap">';
    $paras = paragraphs($p['text'] ?? '');
    if (!empty($paras)) {
        $out .= '<div class="ptext">';
        foreach ($paras as $para) { $out .= '<p>' . nl2br(h($para)) . '</p>'; }
        $out .= '</div>';
    }
    if (!empty($ph)) {
        $n = count($ph);
        $out .= '<div class="gallery' . ($n === 1 ? ' n1' : ($n === 2 ? ' n2' : '')) . '">';
        foreach ($ph as $i => $f) {
            $out .= '<div class="ph"><img src="' . h(photo_url($p['slug'], $f)) . '" alt="' . h($title) . ' foto ' . ($i + 1) . '" loading="lazy" /></div>';
        }
        $out .= '</div>';
    }
    $out .= '</div></main>' . site_footer() . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

/* Pagina met eigen code: de code draait als app.html in een meegroeiende iframe */
function render_code_page($p) {
    $title = page_title($p);
    $t = h($title);
    $out  = site_head($title, meta_desc_for($p), '/' . $p['slug'] . '/');
    $out .= "<style>.page-main{padding:0;} #app-frame{width:100%;border:0;display:block;min-height:100vh;}</style>\n</head>\n<body>\n";
    $out .= site_header('/#projecten', 'Terug naar projecten');
    $out .= <<<HTML
<main class="page-main">
  <iframe id="app-frame" title="{$t}" scrolling="no" loading="eager"></iframe>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  var f = document.getElementById('app-frame');
  function fit(){
    try{
      var d = f.contentDocument || f.contentWindow.document;
      var h = Math.max(d.documentElement ? d.documentElement.scrollHeight : 0, d.body ? d.body.scrollHeight : 0, d.body ? d.body.offsetHeight : 0);
      if (h > 0) { f.style.height = h + 'px'; }
    }catch(e){}
  }
  f.addEventListener('load', function(){
    try{
      var d = f.contentDocument;
      var s = d.createElement('style');
      s.textContent = 'html,body{height:auto !important;overflow:visible !important;}';
      d.head.appendChild(s);
      if (window.ResizeObserver){ var ro = new ResizeObserver(fit); ro.observe(d.documentElement); if (d.body) { ro.observe(d.body); } }
      if (window.MutationObserver && d.body){ new MutationObserver(fit).observe(d.body, {childList:true, subtree:true, attributes:true}); }
    }catch(e){}
    fit();
    var n = 0, iv = setInterval(function(){ fit(); if(++n > 60) { clearInterval(iv); } }, 250);
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize', fit);
  f.src = 'app.html' + window.location.search + window.location.hash;
})();
</script>
HTML;
    $out .= site_footer() . "\n</body>\n</html>\n";
    return $out;
}

/* Schrijft een pagina als index.php met bovenaan de licentiepoort.
   Staat er geen licentie.php (oude installatie), dan doet de poort niets. */
function schrijf_pagina($dir, $html) {
    $pad = (rtrim($dir, '/') === ROOT) ? '__DIR__' : 'dirname(__DIR__)';
    $kop = '<?php $ffp = ' . $pad . " . '/licentie.php'; if (is_file(\$ffp)) { require \$ffp; } ?>";
    file_put_contents($dir . '/index.php', $kop . $html, LOCK_EX);
    @unlink($dir . '/index.html');
}

function write_page_files($p) {
    $dir = ROOT . '/' . $p['slug'];
    if (!is_dir($dir)) { mkdir($dir, 0755, true); }
    if (trim($p['code'] ?? '') !== '') {
        file_put_contents($dir . '/app.html', $p['code']);
        schrijf_pagina($dir, stijl_pagina(render_code_page($p)));
    } else {
        @unlink($dir . '/app.html');
        schrijf_pagina($dir, stijl_pagina(render_project_page($p)));
    }
}

/* ===========================================================
   Alles opnieuw opbouwen na een wijziging
   =========================================================== */
function write_service_pages($pages) {
    global $RESERVED;
    $projectSlugs = array_map(function($p) { return $p['slug']; }, $pages);
    $now = [];
    foreach (services() as $k => $s) {
        if (in_array($s['slug'], $projectSlugs, true) || in_array($s['slug'], $RESERVED, true)) continue;
        $dir = ROOT . '/' . $s['slug'];
        if (!is_dir($dir)) { mkdir($dir, 0755, true); }
        schrijf_pagina($dir, stijl_pagina(render_service_page($k, $pages)));
        $now[] = $s['slug'];
    }
    // Oude dienstpagina's opruimen (alleen mappen die de admin zelf maakte en waar alleen index.html in staat)
    foreach (generated_slugs() as $old) {
        if (in_array($old, $now, true) || in_array($old, $projectSlugs, true) || in_array($old, $RESERVED, true)) continue;
        if (!preg_match('/^[a-z0-9-]+$/', $old)) continue;
        $dir = ROOT . '/' . $old;
        $inhoud = is_dir($dir) ? array_values(array_diff(scandir($dir), ['.', '..'])) : [];
        if ($inhoud === ['index.html'] || $inhoud === ['index.php']) { rrmdir($dir); }
    }
    write_json(GENERATED_FILE, $now);
}
function write_contact_pages() {
    foreach (['contact' => render_contact_page(), 'bedankt' => render_thanks_page()] as $slug => $html) {
        $dir = ROOT . '/' . $slug;
        if (!is_dir($dir)) { mkdir($dir, 0755, true); }
        schrijf_pagina($dir, stijl_pagina($html));
    }
}
function write_seo_files($pages) {
    $u = site_url();
    $today = date('Y-m-d');
    $urls = [['loc' => $u . '/', 'lastmod' => $today]];
    foreach (services() as $s) { $urls[] = ['loc' => $u . '/' . $s['slug'] . '/', 'lastmod' => $today]; }
    $urls[] = ['loc' => $u . '/contact/', 'lastmod' => $today];
    foreach ($pages as $p) {
        if (page_folder($p) === '') continue;
        $d = substr((string)($p['updated'] ?? ($p['created'] ?? '')), 0, 10);
        $urls[] = ['loc' => $u . '/' . rawurlencode($p['slug']) . '/', 'lastmod' => preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $d) ? $d : $today];
    }
    $xml = '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>' . "\n" . '<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">' . "\n";
    foreach ($urls as $x) { $xml .= '  <url><loc>' . h($x['loc']) . '</loc><lastmod>' . $x['lastmod'] . '</lastmod></url>' . "\n"; }
    $xml .= '</urlset>' . "\n";
    file_put_contents(ROOT . '/sitemap.xml', $xml, LOCK_EX);
    $robots = "User-agent: *\nAllow: /\nDisallow: /admin.php\nDisallow: /data/\n\nSitemap: " . $u . "/sitemap.xml\n";
    file_put_contents(ROOT . '/robots.txt', $robots, LOCK_EX);
}
function rebuild_site($pages) {
    list($featured, $projects) = site_lists($pages);
    file_put_contents(SITE_FILE, json_encode(['featured' => $featured, 'projects' => $projects, 'generated' => date('c')], JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES), LOCK_EX);
    schrijf_pagina(ROOT, stijl_pagina(render_home($pages)));
    write_service_pages($pages);
    write_contact_pages();
    write_seo_files($pages);
}
function rebuild_everything($pages) {
    foreach ($pages as $p) { write_page_files($p); }
    rebuild_site($pages);
}

/* ===========================================================
   Licentiepoort (licentie.php)
   De admin schrijft dit bestand zelf. Elke pagina laadt het.
   Het vraagt hooguit eens per 6 uur aan de FormForge-server of
   het abonnement nog loopt. Is de server niet bereikbaar, dan
   blijft de laatst bekende status gelden (bij twijfel: open).
   =========================================================== */
define('POORT_VERSIE', '1');
function poort_code() {
    return <<<'PHP'
<?php
/* FormForge licentiepoort, versie 1. Niet aanpassen: de admin schrijft dit bestand opnieuw. */
if (!function_exists('ff_licentie_status')) {
    function ff_http_get($url, $timeout) {
        if (function_exists('curl_init')) {
            $ch = curl_init($url);
            curl_setopt_array($ch, [CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => $timeout, CURLOPT_CONNECTTIMEOUT => $timeout, CURLOPT_FOLLOWLOCATION => false]);
            $b = curl_exec($ch); $code = (int)curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE); curl_close($ch);
            return ($b !== false && $code >= 200 && $code < 300) ? $b : null;
        }
        $ctx = stream_context_create(['http' => ['timeout' => $timeout, 'ignore_errors' => false]]);
        $b = @file_get_contents($url, false, $ctx);
        return $b === false ? null : $b;
    }
    function ff_licentie_status($vers = false) {
        $dir = __DIR__ . '/data';
        $lic = @json_decode((string)@file_get_contents($dir . '/licentie.json'), true);
        if (!is_array($lic) || empty($lic['key']) || !preg_match('/^[a-f0-9]{48}$/', $lic['key'])) return ['actief' => true, 'geen' => true];
        $cf = $dir . '/licentie-status.json';
        $c = @json_decode((string)@file_get_contents($cf), true);
        if (!is_array($c)) $c = [];
        $nu = time();
        if (!$vers && isset($c['actief']) && $nu - (int)($c['checked'] ?? 0) < 6 * 3600) return $c;
        if (!$vers && isset($c['actief']) && $nu - (int)($c['tried'] ?? 0) < 900) return $c;
        $server = (isset($lic['server']) && preg_match('#^https://[a-z0-9.\-]+$#i', $lic['server'])) ? $lic['server'] : 'https://formforge-sync-1.onrender.com';
        $body = ff_http_get($server . '/api/website/licentie?key=' . rawurlencode($lic['key']) . ($vers ? '&vers=1' : ''), 6);
        $d = $body !== null ? json_decode($body, true) : null;
        if (is_array($d) && isset($d['actief'])) {
            $c = ['actief' => (bool)$d['actief'], 'waarschuwing' => !empty($d['waarschuwing']), 'offlineOp' => (string)($d['offlineOp'] ?? ''), 'reden' => (string)($d['reden'] ?? ''), 'checked' => $nu];
        } elseif (!isset($c['actief'])) {
            $c['actief'] = true;
        }
        $c['tried'] = $nu;
        @file_put_contents($cf, json_encode($c), LOCK_EX);
        return $c;
    }
}
if (!defined('FF_GEEN_POORT')) {
    $ffst = ff_licentie_status(false);
    if (empty($ffst['actief'])) {
        http_response_code(503);
        header('Retry-After: 86400');
        header('Content-Type: text/html; charset=UTF-8');
        echo '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="nl"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><meta name="robots" content="noindex"><title>Tijdelijk niet beschikbaar</title></head>'
           . '<body style="margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#111827;color:#fff;font-family:Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:24px">'
           . '<div><h1 style="font-size:32px;margin:0 0 10px">Deze website is tijdelijk niet beschikbaar.</h1><p style="margin:0;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75);font-size:18px">Probeer het later nog eens.</p></div></body></html>';
        exit;
    }
}
PHP;
}
function schrijf_poort() {
    $code = poort_code();
    $f = ROOT . '/licentie.php';
    if (!is_file($f) || md5_file($f) !== md5($code)) { @file_put_contents($f, $code, LOCK_EX); }
}
function licentie_key() {
    $l = read_json(DATA_DIR . '/licentie.json', []);
    return (isset($l['key']) && preg_match('/^[a-f0-9]{48}$/', $l['key'])) ? $l['key'] : '';
}
function licentie_server() {
    $l = read_json(DATA_DIR . '/licentie.json', []);
    return (isset($l['server']) && preg_match('#^https://[a-z0-9.\-]+$#i', $l['server'])) ? $l['server'] : 'https://formforge-sync-1.onrender.com';
}
function betaal_link() {
    return licentie_server() . '/api/website/betalen?key=' . rawurlencode(licentie_key());
}
function nl_datum($iso) {
    $t = strtotime((string)$iso);
    if (!$t) return '';
    $m = ['januari','februari','maart','april','mei','juni','juli','augustus','september','oktober','november','december'];
    return date('j', $t) . ' ' . $m[(int)date('n', $t) - 1] . ' ' . date('Y', $t);
}

/* Abonnement niet betaald: site offline, beheerpagina dicht */
function toon_geblokkeerd() {
    http_response_code(503);
    $betaal = h(betaal_link());
    $naam = h(st('naam'));
    echo <<<HTML
<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="nl"><head><meta charset="UTF-8" /><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" /><meta name="robots" content="noindex" /><title>Website offline</title>
<style>body{margin:0;min-height:100vh;display:grid;place-items:center;background:#0f1622;color:#eef2f8;font-family:'Segoe UI',Arial,sans-serif;padding:24px;box-sizing:border-box;}
.k{max-width:540px;background:#18212f;border:1px solid #2b384c;border-radius:16px;padding:30px;}
h1{margin:0 0 10px;font-size:26px;} p{margin:0 0 14px;color:#c6d0de;line-height:1.6;}
.r{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px;}
a.b{display:inline-block;background:#2f6bff;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;padding:11px 18px;border-radius:10px;}
a.g{background:transparent;border:1px solid #2b384c;}</style></head>
<body><div class="k"><h1>Je website staat offline</h1>
<p>Het abonnement voor de website van <strong>{$naam}</strong> is niet betaald. Daarom is de website voor bezoekers uitgezet en kun je je beheerpagina niet gebruiken.</p>
<p>Al je teksten, projecten en foto's zijn bewaard. Zodra de betaling rond is, staat alles weer online.</p>
<div class="r"><a class="b" href="{$betaal}">Betaling regelen</a><a class="b g" href="admin.php?controleer=1">Ik heb betaald, controleer opnieuw</a></div>
<p style="margin-top:20px;font-size:14px">Vragen? Mail <a href="mailto:info@formforge.nl" style="color:#8fb3ff">info@formforge.nl</a>.</p></div></body></html>
HTML;
}
/* ===========================================================
   Wachtwoord vergeten
   =========================================================== */
define('RESET_FILE', DATA_DIR . '/reset.json');
function reset_geldig($token) {
    if (!preg_match('/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/', (string)$token)) return false;
    $r = read_json(RESET_FILE, []);
    return !empty($r['hash']) && (int)($r['exp'] ?? 0) > time() && hash_equals($r['hash'], hash('sha256', $token));
}
function stuur_resetmail($naar, $link) {
    // Eerst via de FormForge-server (komt betrouwbaarder aan), anders via de eigen server.
    $key = licentie_key();
    if ($key !== '' && function_exists('curl_init')) {
        $ch = curl_init(licentie_server() . '/api/website/reset-mail');
        curl_setopt_array($ch, [CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 12, CURLOPT_POST => true,
            CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => ['Content-Type: application/json'],
            CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode(['key' => $key, 'email' => $naar, 'link' => $link])]);
        curl_exec($ch); $code = (int)curl_getinfo($ch, CURLINFO_HTTP_CODE); curl_close($ch);
        if ($code >= 200 && $code < 300) return true;
    }
    $host = preg_replace('/:\d+$/', '', preg_replace('/^www\./', '', strtolower((string)($_SERVER['HTTP_HOST'] ?? ''))));
    $van = preg_match('/^[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}$/', $host) ? 'noreply@' . $host : $naar;
    $enc = function($x) { return '=?UTF-8?B?' . base64_encode($x) . '?='; };
    $tekst = "Je hebt gevraagd om een nieuw wachtwoord voor de beheerpagina van " . st('naam') . ".\r\n\r\nKlik op deze link om een nieuw wachtwoord te kiezen:\r\n" . $link . "\r\n\r\nDe link werkt 1 uur. Heb je dit niet zelf gevraagd? Dan kun je deze mail negeren.\r\n";
    $headers = 'From: ' . $enc(st('naam')) . ' <' . $van . ">\r\nMIME-Version: 1.0\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8\r\nContent-Transfer-Encoding: 8bit\r\n";
    return @mail($naar, $enc('Nieuw wachtwoord voor je website'), $tekst, $headers, '-f' . $van);
}
/* ===========================================================
   Inloggen
   =========================================================== */
function login_key() { return hash('sha256', ($_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '') . 'ff-login'); }
function login_blocked() {
    $l = read_json(LOGIN_LIMIT_FILE, []);
    $now = time();
    $recent = array_filter((array)($l[login_key()] ?? []), function($t) use ($now) { return $now - (int)$t < 900; });
    return count($recent) >= 10;
}
function login_failed() {
    $l = read_json(LOGIN_LIMIT_FILE, []);
    $now = time();
    foreach ($l as $k => $v) {
        $l[$k] = array_values(array_filter((array)$v, function($t) use ($now) { return $now - (int)$t < 900; }));
        if (empty($l[$k])) unset($l[$k]);
    }
    $l[login_key()][] = $now;
    write_json(LOGIN_LIMIT_FILE, $l);
}

$config = load_config();
$error = '';
$notice = '';

schrijf_poort();
define('FF_GEEN_POORT', 1);
if (is_file(ROOT . '/licentie.php')) { require_once ROOT . '/licentie.php'; }
$LIC = function_exists('ff_licentie_status') ? ff_licentie_status(isset($_GET['controleer'])) : ['actief' => true];
if ($config && empty($LIC['actief'])) {
    toon_geblokkeerd(); exit;
}

$ADM_EENHEDEN = ['stuk' => 'stuk', 'uur' => 'uur', 'm2' => "m\u{b2}", 'm1' => 'm1', 'dag' => 'dag', 'km' => 'km', 'post' => 'post'];

function adm_voorbeeld() {
    global $is_demo;
    return !empty($is_demo) || defined('ADM_PUBLIEK_DEMO');
}
function adm_inst() {
    $d = ['btwnr' => '', 'iban' => '', 'tnv' => '', 'termijn' => 14, 'geldig' => 30, 'btw' => 21, 'kor' => false,
          'fnr' => 1, 'fjaar' => '', 'onr' => 1, 'ojaar' => '', 'voet_f' => '', 'voet_o' => ''];
    $i = read_json(ADM_INST, []);
    if (adm_voorbeeld()) { $i = ['btwnr' => 'NL001234567B01', 'iban' => 'NL91 ABNA 0417 1643 00', 'tnv' => st('naam'), 'fnr' => 5, 'fjaar' => date('Y'), 'onr' => 3, 'ojaar' => date('Y')]; }
    return array_merge($d, $i);
}
function adm_klanten() {
    if (adm_voorbeeld()) return adm_demo()['klanten'];
    return read_json(ADM_KLANTEN, []);
}
function adm_docs() {
    if (adm_voorbeeld()) return adm_demo()['docs'];
    return read_json(ADM_DOCS, []);
}
function adm_save_klanten($k) { write_json(ADM_KLANTEN, array_values($k)); }
function adm_save_docs($d) { write_json(ADM_DOCS, array_values($d)); }
function adm_id($p) { return $p . bin2hex(random_bytes(6)); }
function adm_vind($lijst, $id) {
    foreach ($lijst as $i => $x) { if (($x['id'] ?? '') === $id) return $i; }
    return null;
}

/* ---------- rekenen en opmaak ---------- */
function adm_getal($s) {
    $s = str_replace([' ', "\xC2\xA0", "\u{20ac}"], '', trim((string)$s));
    if (strpos($s, ',') !== false) { $s = str_replace('.', '', $s); $s = str_replace(',', '.', $s); }
    return is_numeric($s) ? (float)$s : 0.0;
}
function adm_cent($s) { return (int)round(adm_getal($s) * 100); }
function adm_eur($c) { return ($c < 0 ? '- ' : '') . "\u{20AC} " . number_format(abs($c) / 100, 2, ',', '.'); }
function adm_invoer($c) { return $c === 0 ? '' : number_format($c / 100, 2, ',', ''); }
function adm_aantal($a) {
    $t = number_format((float)$a, 2, ',', '.');
    return preg_replace('/,?0+$/', '', $t) ?: '0';
}
function adm_tot($d) {
    if (!empty($d['vast'])) {
        $v = $d['vast'];
        return ['excl' => (int)$v['excl'], 'groepen' => [(int)$v['pct'] => ['grond' => (int)$v['excl'], 'btw' => (int)$v['btw']]], 'btw' => (int)$v['btw'], 'incl' => (int)$v['excl'] + (int)$v['btw']];
    }
    $groepen = []; $excl = 0;
    foreach (($d['regels'] ?? []) as $r) {
        $b = (int)round((float)$r['aantal'] * (int)$r['prijs']);
        $t = empty($d['kor']) ? (int)$r['btw'] : 0;
        if (!isset($groepen[$t])) { $groepen[$t] = ['grond' => 0, 'btw' => 0]; }
        $groepen[$t]['grond'] += $b; $excl += $b;
    }
    $btw = 0;
    foreach ($groepen as $t => $g) { $groepen[$t]['btw'] = (int)round($g['grond'] * $t / 100); $btw += $groepen[$t]['btw']; }
    krsort($groepen);
    return ['excl' => $excl, 'groepen' => $groepen, 'btw' => $btw, 'incl' => $excl + $btw];
}
function adm_verval($d) { return date('Y-m-d', strtotime(($d['datum'] ?: date('Y-m-d')) . ' +' . (int)($d['termijn'] ?? 14) . ' days')); }
function adm_status($d) {
    $vandaag = date('Y-m-d');
    if ($d['soort'] === 'offerte') {
        if ($d['status'] === 'akkoord') return ['akkoord', 'Akkoord'];
        if ($d['status'] === 'afgewezen') return ['afgewezen', 'Afgewezen'];
        if ($d['geldig_tot'] !== '' && $d['geldig_tot'] < $vandaag) return ['verlopen', 'Verlopen'];
        if ($d['status'] === 'verstuurd') return ['verstuurd', 'Verstuurd'];
        return ['concept', 'Concept'];
    }
    if ($d['status'] === 'concept') return ['concept', 'Concept'];
    if ($d['status'] === 'betaald') return ['betaald', !empty($d['credit']) ? 'Verrekend' : 'Betaald'];
    if (adm_verval($d) < $vandaag) return ['telaat', 'Te laat'];
    return ['open', 'Open'];
}
function adm_titel($d) {
    if ($d['soort'] === 'offerte') return 'Offerte';
    return !empty($d['credit']) ? 'Creditfactuur' : 'Factuur';
}
function adm_naam($d) {
    return adm_titel($d) . ($d['nummer'] !== '' ? ' ' . $d['nummer'] : ' (concept)');
}
function adm_volgnummer(&$inst, $soort) {
    $j = date('Y');
    if ($soort === 'factuur') {
        if ((string)$inst['fjaar'] !== $j) { if ($inst['fjaar'] !== '') { $inst['fnr'] = 1; } $inst['fjaar'] = $j; }
        $nr = $j . '-' . str_pad((string)(int)$inst['fnr'], 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
        $inst['fnr'] = (int)$inst['fnr'] + 1;
    } else {
        if ((string)$inst['ojaar'] !== $j) { if ($inst['ojaar'] !== '') { $inst['onr'] = 1; } $inst['ojaar'] = $j; }
        $nr = $j . '-O' . str_pad((string)(int)$inst['onr'], 3, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
        $inst['onr'] = (int)$inst['onr'] + 1;
    }
    return $nr;
}
/* Adres van de admin voor links in mails: altijd https bij een echte domeinnaam */
function adm_basis() {
    $u = site_url();
    if (stripos($u, 'http://') === 0 && !preg_match('#^http://(localhost|127\.|\[::1\])#i', $u)) { $u = 'https://' . substr($u, 7); }
    return $u . rtrim(str_replace('\\', '/', dirname((string)($_SERVER['SCRIPT_NAME'] ?? '/admin.php'))), '/') . '/admin.php';
}
function adm_link($d) {
    return adm_basis() . '?doc=' . rawurlencode($d['token']);
}
function adm_mailtekst($d, $inst) {
    $t = adm_tot($d); $st = adm_status($d);
    $groet = "\n\nMet vriendelijke groet,\n" . st('naam') . (st('tel') !== '' ? "\n" . st('tel') : '');
    $aan = 'Beste ' . (($d['klant']['contact'] ?? '') !== '' ? $d['klant']['contact'] : $d['klant']['naam']) . ',';
    if ($d['soort'] === 'offerte') {
        return [adm_naam($d) . ' van ' . st('naam'),
            $aan . "\n\nHierbij ontvangt u onze offerte " . $d['nummer'] . ($d['onderwerp'] !== '' ? ' voor ' . $d['onderwerp'] : '') . '. Het totaalbedrag is ' . adm_eur($t['incl']) . (empty($d['kor']) ? ' inclusief btw' : '') . '.'
            . "\n\nU bekijkt de offerte via de link hieronder. Daar kunt u hem ook bewaren als pdf en direct akkoord geven:\n" . adm_link($d)
            . ($d['geldig_tot'] !== '' ? "\n\nDe offerte is geldig tot en met " . nl_datum($d['geldig_tot']) . '.' : '') . "\n\nHeeft u vragen? Beantwoord gerust deze mail." . $groet];
    }
    $betaal = $inst['iban'] !== '' ? ' op ' . $inst['iban'] . ($inst['tnv'] !== '' ? ' ten name van ' . $inst['tnv'] : '') . ', onder vermelding van ' . $d['nummer'] : '';
    if (!empty($d['credit'])) {
        return [adm_naam($d) . ' van ' . st('naam'), $aan . "\n\nHierbij ontvangt u creditfactuur " . $d['nummer'] . ' van ' . adm_eur(abs($t['incl'])) . ".\n\nU bekijkt de creditfactuur hier:\n" . adm_link($d) . $groet];
    }
    if ($st[0] === 'telaat') {
        return ['Herinnering: ' . adm_naam($d) . ' van ' . st('naam'),
            $aan . "\n\nVolgens onze administratie is factuur " . $d['nummer'] . ' van ' . nl_datum($d['datum']) . ' nog niet betaald. De betaaltermijn is verlopen op ' . nl_datum(adm_verval($d)) . '.'
            . "\n\nWilt u het bedrag van " . adm_eur($t['incl']) . ' zo snel mogelijk overmaken' . $betaal . '?'
            . "\n\nU bekijkt de factuur hier:\n" . adm_link($d) . "\n\nHeeft u inmiddels betaald? Dan kunt u deze mail als niet verzonden beschouwen." . $groet];
    }
    return [adm_naam($d) . ' van ' . st('naam'),
        $aan . "\n\nHierbij ontvangt u factuur " . $d['nummer'] . ($d['onderwerp'] !== '' ? ' voor ' . $d['onderwerp'] : '') . '.'
        . "\n\nWilt u het bedrag van " . adm_eur($t['incl']) . ' voor ' . nl_datum(adm_verval($d)) . ' overmaken' . $betaal . '?'
        . "\n\nU bekijkt en bewaart de factuur (ook als pdf) via deze link:\n" . adm_link($d) . $groet];
}

/* ---------- het document zelf (scherm, pdf en afdruk) ---------- */
function adm_papier($d, $inst) {
    global $ADM_EENHEDEN;
    $t = adm_tot($d); $k = $d['klant']; $st = adm_status($d);
    $accent = valid_color(st('kleur_accent'), '#1d4ed8');
    $kor = !empty($d['kor']);
    ob_start(); ?>
<div class="papier" style="--acc:<?= h($accent) ?>">
  <div class="p-kop">
    <div class="p-van">
      <?php $dlogo = adm_logo_doc()[1]; if ($dlogo !== ''): ?><img class="p-logo" src="<?= h($dlogo) ?>" alt="<?= h(st('naam')) ?>" /><?php else: ?><div class="p-naam"><?= h(st('naam')) ?></div><?php endif; ?>
    </div>
    <div class="p-gegevens">

      <?php if (st('kvk') !== ''): ?>KvK <?= h(st('kvk')) ?><br /><?php endif; ?>
      <?php if ($inst['btwnr'] !== '' && !$kor): ?>Btw <?= h($inst['btwnr']) ?><br /><?php endif; ?>
      <?php if ($inst['iban'] !== '' && $d['soort'] === 'factuur'): ?>IBAN <?= h($inst['iban']) ?><?php endif; ?>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="p-mid">

    </div>
    <div class="p-meta">
      <h1><?= h(adm_titel($d)) ?><?php if ($st[0] === 'concept' && $d['soort'] === 'factuur'): ?> <span class="p-stempel">Concept</span><?php elseif ($st[0] === 'betaald'): ?> <span class="p-stempel p-ok"><?= h($st[1]) ?></span><?php endif; ?></h1>
      <table>
        <tr><td><?= $d['soort'] === 'offerte' ? 'Offertenummer' : 'Factuurnummer' ?></td><td><?= $d['nummer'] !== '' ? h($d['nummer']) : 'volgt' ?></td></tr>
        <tr><td><?= $d['soort'] === 'offerte' ? 'Datum' : 'Factuurdatum' ?></td><td><?= h(nl_datum($d['datum'])) ?></td></tr>
        <?php if ($d['soort'] === 'factuur' && empty($d['credit'])): ?><tr><td>Vervaldatum</td><td><?= h(nl_datum(adm_verval($d))) ?></td></tr><?php endif; ?>
        <?php if ($d['soort'] === 'factuur' && $d['leverdatum'] !== ''): ?><tr><td>Datum levering</td><td><?= h(nl_datum($d['leverdatum'])) ?></td></tr><?php endif; ?>
        <?php if ($d['soort'] === 'offerte' && $d['geldig_tot'] !== ''): ?><tr><td>Geldig tot</td><td><?= h(nl_datum($d['geldig_tot'])) ?></td></tr><?php endif; ?>
      </table>
    </div>

    </tbody>
  </table>

      <?php endforeach; ?>
      <tr class="p-eind"><td>Totaal inclusief btw</td><td class="r"><?= h(adm_eur($t['incl'])) ?></td></tr>
    <?php endif; ?>
  </table>
  <?php if ($kor): ?><p class="p-klein">Btw niet van toepassing: vrijgesteld op grond van de kleineondernemersregeling (KOR).</p><?php endif; ?>

  <div class="p-voet">
    <?php if ($d['soort'] === 'factuur' && !empty($d['credit'])): ?>
      <p>Dit bedrag verrekenen wij met u of storten wij terug.</p>

'factuurnummer <strong>' . h($d['nummer']) . '</strong>' : 'het factuurnummer' ?>.</p>
