/* ---------- inkoopfacturen ---------- */
function adm_ink_status($x) {
    if ($x['status'] === 'genegeerd') return ['genegeerd', 'Genegeerd'];
    if ($x['status'] === 'betaald') return ['betaald', 'Betaald'];
    $dagen = (int)floor((strtotime($x['verval']) - strtotime(date('Y-m-d'))) / 86400);
    if ($dagen < 0) return ['telaat', abs($dagen) . ($dagen === -1 ? ' dag' : ' dagen') . ' te laat'];
    if ($dagen === 0) return ['open', 'Vandaag betalen'];
    return ['open', 'Nog ' . $dagen . ($dagen === 1 ? ' dag' : ' dagen')];
}
function adm_pdf_bewaar($bin) {
    if (substr($bin, 0, 5) !== '%PDF-' || strlen($bin) > 20 * 1024 * 1024) return '';
    ensure_data_dir();
    if (!is_dir(ADM_PDF_DIR)) { mkdir(ADM_PDF_DIR, 0755, true); }
    $naam = date('Ymd') . '-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(8)) . '.pdf';
    file_put_contents(ADM_PDF_DIR . '/' . $naam, $bin, LOCK_EX);
    return $naam;
}
function adm_pdf_pad($naam) {
    return preg_match('/^[0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{16}\.pdf$/', (string)$naam) ? ADM_PDF_DIR . '/' . $naam : '';
}

/* Tekst uit een pdf halen. Werkt bij de meeste pdf's die door een programma zijn gemaakt.
   Een pdf die alleen een plaatje (scan) is, levert geen tekst op. */
function adm_a85($d) {
    $d = preg_replace('/\s+/', '', $d);
    if (strpos($d, '<~') === 0) { $d = substr($d, 2); }
    $e = strpos($d, '~>'); if ($e !== false) { $d = substr($d, 0, $e); }
    $uit = ''; $groep = '';
    $n = strlen($d);
    for ($i = 0; $i < $n; $i++) {
        $c = $d[$i];
        if ($c === 'z' && $groep === '') { $uit .= "\0\0\0\0"; continue; }
        $groep .= $c;
        if (strlen($groep) === 5) {
            $v = 0; for ($j = 0; $j < 5; $j++) { $v = $v * 85 + (ord($groep[$j]) - 33); }
            $uit .= pack('N', $v & 0xFFFFFFFF); $groep = '';
        }
    }
    if ($groep !== '') {
        $len = strlen($groep); $groep = str_pad($groep, 5, 'u');
        $v = 0; for ($j = 0; $j < 5; $j++) { $v = $v * 85 + (ord($groep[$j]) - 33); }
        $uit .= substr(pack('N', $v & 0xFFFFFFFF), 0, $len - 1);
    }
    return $uit;
}
function adm_utf16hex($h) {
    $b = @hex2bin(strlen($h) % 2 ? '0' . $h : $h);
    if ($b === false) return '';
    if (strlen($b) === 1) return mb_convert_encoding($b, 'UTF-8', 'ISO-8859-1');
    return (string)@mb_convert_encoding($b, 'UTF-8', 'UTF-16BE');
}
function adm_pdf_tekst($pdf) {
    @set_time_limit(60);
    /* 1. alle objecten met hun (uitgepakte) stream */
    $obj = []; $str = [];
    $pos = 0; $len = strlen($pdf);
    while (preg_match('/(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+obj\b/', $pdf, $m, PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE, $pos)) {
        $id = (int)$m[1][0];
        $begin = $m[0][1] + strlen($m[0][0]);
        $eind = strpos($pdf, 'endobj', $begin);
        if ($eind === false) break;
        $sp = strpos($pdf, 'stream', $begin);
        if ($sp !== false && $sp < $eind) {
            $dict = substr($pdf, $begin, $sp - $begin);
            $start = $sp + 6;
            if (substr($pdf, $start, 2) === "\r\n") { $start += 2; } elseif (isset($pdf[$start]) && ($pdf[$start] === "\n" || $pdf[$start] === "\r")) { $start++; }
            $lengte = preg_match('#/Length\s+(\d++)(?!\s+\d+\s+R)#', $dict, $lm) ? (int)$lm[1] : -1;
            $es = strpos($pdf, 'endstream', $start);
            if ($es === false) break;
            if ($lengte >= 0 && $start + $lengte <= $es) { $data = substr($pdf, $start, $lengte); } else { $data = rtrim(substr($pdf, $start, $es - $start), "\r\n"); }
            $eind = strpos($pdf, 'endobj', $es);
            if ($eind === false) break;
            $obj[$id] = $dict;
            $str[$id] = adm_pdf_filters($dict, $data);
        } else {
            $obj[$id] = substr($pdf, $begin, $eind - $begin);
        }
        $pos = $eind + 6;
        if (count($obj) > 20000) break;
    }
    /* objecten die in een objectstream zitten (nieuwere pdf's) */
    foreach ($obj as $id => $dict) {
        if (strpos($dict, '/ObjStm') === false || empty($str[$id])) continue;
        $first = preg_match('#/First\s+(\d+)#', $dict, $fm) ? (int)$fm[1] : 0;
        $n = preg_match('#/N\s+(\d+)#', $dict, $nm) ? (int)$nm[1] : 0;
        $kop = preg_split('/\s+/', trim(substr($str[$id], 0, $first)));
        for ($k = 0; $k + 1 < count($kop) && $k / 2 < $n; $k += 2) {
            $oid = (int)$kop[$k]; $off = (int)$kop[$k + 1];
            $volgende = isset($kop[$k + 3]) ? (int)$kop[$k + 3] : strlen($str[$id]) - $first;
            if (!isset($obj[$oid])) { $obj[$oid] = substr($str[$id], $first + $off, $volgende - $off); }
        }
    }
    /* 2. lettertypen: naam in de pagina => ToUnicode-tabel */
    $cmaps = [];
    $fontInfo = function($fid) use (&$obj, &$str, &$cmaps) {
        if (isset($cmaps[$fid])) return $cmaps[$fid];
        $d = $obj[$fid] ?? '';
        $info = ['map' => [], 'twee' => strpos($d, 'Identity-H') !== false || strpos($d, 'Type0') !== false, 'w' => [], 'dw' => 0];
        /* breedtes van de letters, voor de spaties tussen woorden */
        if (preg_match('#/Widths\s*\[([^\]]*)\]#', $d, $wm) && preg_match('#/FirstChar\s+(\d+)#', $d, $fc)) {
            foreach (preg_split('/\s+/', trim($wm[1])) as $k => $w) { if (is_numeric($w)) $info['w'][(int)$fc[1] + $k] = (float)$w; }
        } elseif (preg_match('#/Widths\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $d, $wr) && preg_match('#/FirstChar\s+(\d+)#', $d, $fc) && isset($obj[(int)$wr[1]])) {
            foreach (preg_split('/\s+/', trim($obj[(int)$wr[1]], " \t\r\n[]")) as $k => $w) { if (is_numeric($w)) $info['w'][(int)$fc[1] + $k] = (float)$w; }
        }
        if (preg_match('#/DescendantFonts\s*\[?\s*(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $d, $dm) && isset($obj[(int)$dm[1]])) {
            $cd = $obj[(int)$dm[1]];
            $info['dw'] = preg_match('#/DW\s+(\d+)#', $cd, $dwm) ? (float)$dwm[1] : 1000;
            $wtxt = preg_match('#/W\s*\[(.*)\]#s', $cd, $wm) ? $wm[1] : (preg_match('#/W\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $cd, $wr) ? trim($obj[(int)$wr[1]] ?? '', " \t\r\n[]") : '');
            if ($wtxt !== '' && preg_match_all('/\[[^\]]*\]|[\d.]+/', $wtxt, $tk)) {
                $t = $tk[0]; $k = 0;
                while ($k < count($t)) {
                    $c1 = (int)$t[$k];
                    if (isset($t[$k + 1]) && $t[$k + 1][0] === '[') {
                        foreach (preg_split('/\s+/', trim($t[$k + 1], '[] ')) as $o => $w) { if (is_numeric($w)) $info['w'][$c1 + $o] = (float)$w; }
                        $k += 2;
                    } elseif (isset($t[$k + 2])) {
                        for ($c = $c1; $c <= (int)$t[$k + 1] && $c - $c1 < 5000; $c++) { $info['w'][$c] = (float)$t[$k + 2]; }
                        $k += 3;
                    } else break;
                }
            }
        }
        if (preg_match('#/ToUnicode\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $d, $tm) && !empty($str[(int)$tm[1]])) {
            $info['map'] = adm_pdf_cmap($str[(int)$tm[1]]);
            foreach (array_keys($info['map']) as $k) { $info['twee'] = strlen($k) === 4; break; }
        }
        return $cmaps[$fid] = $info;
    };
    $fonts = [];
    foreach ($obj as $id => $d) {
        if (preg_match_all('#/Font\s*<<(.*?)>>#s', $d, $fm)) {
            foreach ($fm[1] as $blok) { if (preg_match_all('#/([^\s/<>\[\]()]+)\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $blok, $pp, PREG_SET_ORDER)) { foreach ($pp as $p) { $fonts[$p[1]] = (int)$p[2]; } } }
        }
        if (preg_match('#/Font\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $d, $fr) && isset($obj[(int)$fr[1]])) {
            if (preg_match_all('#/([^\s/<>\[\]()]+)\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $obj[(int)$fr[1]], $pp, PREG_SET_ORDER)) { foreach ($pp as $p) { $fonts[$p[1]] = (int)$p[2]; } }
        }
    }
    /* 3. inhoud van de pagina's in volgorde */
    $inhoud = [];
    foreach ($obj as $id => $d) {
        if (!preg_match('#/Type\s*/Page\b#', $d)) continue;
        if (preg_match('#/Contents\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $d, $cm)) { $inhoud[] = (int)$cm[1]; }
        elseif (preg_match('#/Contents\s*\[([^\]]*)\]#', $d, $cm) && preg_match_all('#(\d+)\s+\d+\s+R#', $cm[1], $rr)) { foreach ($rr[1] as $r) { $inhoud[] = (int)$r; } }
    }
    if (!$inhoud) { foreach ($str as $id => $s) { if (strpos($s, 'BT') !== false && strpos($s, 'begincmap') === false) $inhoud[] = $id; } }
    $tekst = '';
    foreach ($inhoud as $cid) {
        if (empty($str[$cid])) continue;
        $tekst .= adm_pdf_inhoud($str[$cid], $fonts, $fontInfo) . "\n";
    }
    $tekst = preg_replace('/[\x{2010}-\x{2015}\x{2212}\x{00AD}]/u', '-', $tekst);
    $tekst = preg_replace('/(?<=[A-Za-z0-9])[\x{E000}-\x{F8FF}](?=[A-Za-z0-9])/u', '-', $tekst);
    $tekst = preg_replace('/[\x{E000}-\x{F8FF}]/u', '', $tekst);
    $tekst = preg_replace('/[ \t\x{00A0}]+/u', ' ', $tekst);
    $tekst = preg_replace('/ *\n\s*/', "\n", $tekst);
    return trim((string)$tekst);
}
function adm_pdf_filters($dict, $data) {
    if (preg_match('#/(DCTDecode|JPXDecode|CCITTFaxDecode|JBIG2Decode)#', $dict)) return '';
    if (!preg_match('#/Filter\s*(\[[^\]]*\]|/\w+)#', $dict, $fm)) return $data;
    preg_match_all('#/(\w+)#', $fm[1], $filters);
    foreach ($filters[1] as $flt) {
        if ($flt === 'ASCII85Decode' || $flt === 'A85') { $data = adm_a85($data); }
        elseif ($flt === 'ASCIIHexDecode' || $flt === 'AHx') { $hx = preg_replace('/[^0-9A-Fa-f]/', '', $data); $data = (string)@hex2bin(strlen($hx) % 2 ? $hx . '0' : $hx); }
        elseif ($flt === 'FlateDecode' || $flt === 'Fl') {
            $u = @gzuncompress($data);
            if ($u === false) { $u = @gzinflate(substr($data, 2)); }
            if ($u === false) { $u = @gzinflate($data); }
            $data = $u === false ? '' : $u;
        } else { return ''; }
        if ($data === '') return '';
    }
    return $data;
}
function adm_pdf_cmap($s) {
    $map = [];
    if (preg_match_all('/beginbfchar(.*?)endbfchar/s', $s, $mm)) {
        foreach ($mm[1] as $blok) {
            if (preg_match_all('/<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*<([0-9A-Fa-f]*)>/', $blok, $pp, PREG_SET_ORDER)) { foreach ($pp as $x) { $map[strtoupper($x[1])] = adm_utf16hex($x[2]); } }
        }
    }
    if (preg_match_all('/beginbfrange(.*?)endbfrange/s', $s, $mm)) {
        foreach ($mm[1] as $blok) {
            if (!preg_match_all('/<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*(<[0-9A-Fa-f]+>|\[[^\]]*\])/', $blok, $pp, PREG_SET_ORDER)) continue;
            foreach ($pp as $x) {
                $a = hexdec($x[1]); $b = hexdec($x[2]); $l = strlen($x[1]);
                if ($b < $a || $b - $a > 5000) continue;
                if ($x[3][0] === '<') {
                    $h = trim($x[3], '<>'); $doel = hexdec(substr($h, -4)); $voor = substr($h, 0, -4);
                    for ($c = $a; $c <= $b; $c++) { $map[strtoupper(str_pad(dechex($c), $l, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT))] = adm_utf16hex($voor . str_pad(dechex($doel + $c - $a), 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT)); }
                } elseif (preg_match_all('/<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>/', $x[3], $dd)) {
                    foreach ($dd[1] as $k => $h) { $map[strtoupper(str_pad(dechex($a + $k), $l, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT))] = adm_utf16hex($h); }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return $map;
}
/* De tekstopdrachten van een pagina lezen en de regels in de goede volgorde zetten */
function adm_pdf_inhoud($s, $fonts, $fontInfo) {
    $n = strlen($s); $i = 0; $ops = [];
    $uit = '';
    $font = ['map' => [], 'twee' => false, 'w' => [], 'dw' => 0]; $size = 10; $sx = 1; $sy = 1; $lead = 0;
    $lx = 0; $ly = 0; $x = 0; $y = 0; $laatsteY = null; $laatsteEind = null;
    $zet = function($t, $breedte = null) use (&$uit, &$x, &$y, &$laatsteY, &$laatsteEind, &$size, &$sx, &$sy) {
        if ($t === '') { if ($breedte !== null) { $x += $breedte * abs($size * $sx); } return; }
        $h = abs($size * $sy) ?: 10; $b = abs($size * $sx) ?: 10;
        if ($laatsteY !== null) {
            if (abs($y - $laatsteY) > $h * 0.45) { $uit .= "\n"; }
            elseif ($laatsteEind !== null && ($x > $laatsteEind + $b * 0.22 || $x < $laatsteEind - $b * 3)) { $uit .= ' '; }
        }
        $uit .= $t;
        $laatsteY = $y;
        if ($breedte === null) {
            $breedte = 0;
            foreach (preg_split('//u', $t, -1, PREG_SPLIT_NO_EMPTY) as $ch) { $breedte += strpos('il.,:;|!\'tfjr ', $ch) !== false ? 0.3 : (strpos('mwMW', $ch) !== false ? 0.85 : (ctype_upper($ch) ? 0.68 : 0.52)); }
        }
        $x += $breedte * $b;
        $laatsteEind = $x;
    };
    $decode = function($bytes) use (&$font) { return adm_pdf_str($bytes, $font['map'], $font['twee']); };
    $breed = function($bytes) use (&$font) {
        if (empty($font['w']) && empty($font['dw'])) return null;
        $stap = $font['twee'] ? 2 : 1; $som = 0;
        for ($i = 0; $i < strlen($bytes); $i += $stap) {
            $code = $stap === 2 ? (ord($bytes[$i]) << 8) + ord($bytes[$i + 1] ?? "\0") : ord($bytes[$i]);
            $som += $font['w'][$code] ?? ($font['dw'] ?: 500);
        }
        return $som / 1000;
    };
    while ($i < $n) {
        $c = $s[$i];
        if (ctype_space($c)) { $i++; continue; }
        if ($c === '%') { $e = strpos($s, "\n", $i); $i = $e === false ? $n : $e + 1; continue; }
        if ($c === '(') {
            $d = 1; $j = $i + 1; $buf = '';
            while ($j < $n && $d > 0) {
                $ch = $s[$j];
                if ($ch === '\\') { $buf .= $ch . ($s[$j + 1] ?? ''); $j += 2; continue; }
                if ($ch === '(') $d++;
                if ($ch === ')') { $d--; if ($d === 0) break; }
                $buf .= $ch; $j++;
            }
            $ops[] = ['s', adm_pdf_literal($buf)]; $i = $j + 1; continue;
        }
        if ($c === '<') {
            if (($s[$i + 1] ?? '') === '<') { $i += 2; continue; }
            $e = strpos($s, '>', $i); if ($e === false) break;
            $hx = preg_replace('/[^0-9A-Fa-f]/', '', substr($s, $i + 1, $e - $i - 1));
            $ops[] = ['s', (string)@hex2bin(strlen($hx) % 2 ? $hx . '0' : $hx)]; $i = $e + 1; continue;
        }
        if ($c === '>') { $i++; continue; }
        if ($c === '[') { $ops[] = ['['] ; $i++; continue; }
        if ($c === ']') {
            $arr = [];
            while ($ops && $ops[count($ops) - 1][0] !== '[') { array_unshift($arr, array_pop($ops)); }
            array_pop($ops); $ops[] = ['a', $arr]; $i++; continue;
        }
        if ($c === '/') { preg_match('#/[^\s/\[\]()<>{}%]*#A', $s, $m, 0, $i); $ops[] = ['n', substr($m[0], 1)]; $i += strlen($m[0]); continue; }
        if (preg_match('/[+\-.\d]/A', $c)) { preg_match('/[+\-]?(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)/A', $s, $m, 0, $i); if (!$m) { $i++; continue; } $ops[] = ['g', (float)$m[0]]; $i += strlen($m[0]); continue; }
        if (!preg_match('/[A-Za-z\'"*]+/A', $s, $m, 0, $i)) { $i++; continue; }
        $op = $m[0]; $i += strlen($op);
        $g = function($k) use ($ops) { $c = count($ops); return isset($ops[$c - $k]) && $ops[$c - $k][0] === 'g' ? $ops[$c - $k][1] : 0; };
        switch ($op) {
            case 'BI': $e = strpos($s, 'EI', $i); $i = $e === false ? $n : $e + 2; break;
            case 'BT': $lx = $x = 0; $ly = $y = 0; $sx = $sy = 1; break;
            case 'Tf':
                $c2 = count($ops); $naam = $ops[$c2 - 2][0] === 'n' ? $ops[$c2 - 2][1] : '';
                $size = $g(1) ?: $size;
                $font = isset($fonts[$naam]) ? $fontInfo($fonts[$naam]) : ['map' => [], 'twee' => false, 'w' => [], 'dw' => 0];
                break;
            case 'TL': $lead = $g(1); break;
            case 'Td': case 'TD':
                $tx = $g(2); $ty = $g(1);
                if ($op === 'TD') { $lead = -$ty; }
                $lx += $tx * $sx; $ly += $ty * $sy; $x = $lx; $y = $ly; break;
            case 'Tm':
                $sx = $g(6) ?: 1; $sy = $g(3) ?: 1; $lx = $x = $g(2); $ly = $y = $g(1); break;
            case 'T*': $ly -= $lead * $sy; $x = $lx; $y = $ly; break;
            case 'Tj': case "'": case '"':
                if ($op !== 'Tj') { $ly -= $lead * $sy; $x = $lx; $y = $ly; }
                $c2 = count($ops);
                if ($c2 && $ops[$c2 - 1][0] === 's') { $zet($decode($ops[$c2 - 1][1]), $breed($ops[$c2 - 1][1])); }
                break;
            case 'TJ':
                $c2 = count($ops);
                if ($c2 && $ops[$c2 - 1][0] === 'a') {
                    foreach ($ops[$c2 - 1][1] as $el) {
                        if ($el[0] === 's') { $zet($decode($el[1]), $breed($el[1])); }
                        elseif ($el[0] === 'g') { $x -= $el[1] / 1000 * abs($size * $sx); }
                    }
                }
                break;
        }
        $ops = [];
    }
    return $uit;
}
function adm_pdf_literal($s) {
    return preg_replace_callback('/\\\\([nrtbf()\\\\]|[0-7]{1,3}|\r?\n)/', function($m) {
        $x = $m[1];
        $vast = ['n' => "\n", 'r' => "\r", 't' => "\t", 'b' => "\x08", 'f' => "\x0C", '(' => '(', ')' => ')', '\\' => '\\'];
        if (isset($vast[$x])) return $vast[$x];
        if ($x[0] === "\r" || $x[0] === "\n") return '';
        return chr(octdec($x) & 255);
    }, $s);
}
function adm_pdf_str($bytes, $map, $twee) {
    if ($bytes === '') return '';
    if ($map) {
        $uit = '';
        $stap = $twee ? 2 : 1;
        $l = strlen($bytes);
        for ($i = 0; $i < $l; $i += $stap) {
            $k = strtoupper(bin2hex(substr($bytes, $i, $stap)));
            $uit .= $map[$k] ?? '';
        }
        return $uit;
    }
    if ($twee) return '';
    $u = mb_convert_encoding($bytes, 'UTF-8', 'Windows-1252');
    return preg_replace('/[\x00-\x08\x0B\x0C\x0E-\x1F]/', '', $u);
}
function adm_bedrag_uit($s) {
    $s = str_replace(' ', '', $s);
    if (preg_match('/^-?\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})*,\d{2}$/', $s) || preg_match('/^-?\d+,\d{2}$/', $s)) return (int)round((float)str_replace(',', '.', str_replace('.', '', $s)) * 100);
    if (preg_match('/^-?\d{1,3}(,\d{3})*\.\d{2}$/', $s) || preg_match('/^-?\d+\.\d{2}$/', $s)) return (int)round((float)str_replace(',', '', $s) * 100);
    return null;
}
function adm_datum_uit($s) {
    $maanden = ['jan' => 1, 'feb' => 2, 'mrt' => 3, 'maa' => 3, 'mar' => 3, "m\u{e4}r" => 3, 'apr' => 4, 'mei' => 5, 'may' => 5, 'mai' => 5, 'jun' => 6, 'jul' => 7, 'aug' => 8, 'sep' => 9, 'okt' => 10, 'oct' => 10, 'nov' => 11, 'dec' => 12, 'dez' => 12];
    if (preg_match('/\b(\d{1,2})[-.\/](\d{1,2})[-.\/](\d{4}|\d{2})\b/', $s, $m)) {
        $j = strlen($m[3]) === 2 ? 2000 + (int)$m[3] : (int)$m[3];
        if (checkdate((int)$m[2], (int)$m[1], $j)) return sprintf('%04d-%02d-%02d', $j, $m[2], $m[1]);
    }
    if (preg_match('/\b(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})\b/', $s, $m) && checkdate((int)$m[2], (int)$m[3], (int)$m[1])) return $m[1] . '-' . $m[2] . '-' . $m[3];
    if (preg_match("/\\b(\\d{1,2})\\.?\\s+([a-z\u{e4}A-Z]{3,9})\\.?\\s+(\\d{4})\\b/u", $s, $m)) {
        $k = mb_strtolower(mb_substr($m[2], 0, 3));
        if (isset($maanden[$k]) && checkdate($maanden[$k], (int)$m[1], (int)$m[3])) return sprintf('%04d-%02d-%02d', $m[3], $maanden[$k], $m[1]);
    }
    return '';
}
/* Gegevens van een inkoopfactuur raden uit de tekst. Altijd laten controleren. */
function adm_pdf_velden($tekst) {
    $r = ['datum' => '', 'nummer' => '', 'bedrag' => null, 'btw' => null, 'verlegd' => false];
    $tekst = trim(preg_replace("/[ \t]*\r?\n\s*/", "\n", (string)$tekst));
    if ($tekst === '') return $r;
    $regels = preg_split('/\n/', $tekst);
    $bedragRe = '/(?<![\d,.])-?(?:\d{1,3}(?:[.\s]\d{3})+|\d+)[,.]\d{2}(?![\d])/';
    $alle = [];
    foreach ($regels as $i => $rg) {
        if (preg_match_all($bedragRe, $rg, $m)) { foreach ($m[0] as $b) { $v = adm_bedrag_uit($b); if ($v !== null) { $alle[] = [$i, $v]; } } }
    }
    $bedragOp = function($i) use ($regels, $bedragRe) {
        foreach ([$i, $i + 1, $i + 2] as $j) {
            if (!isset($regels[$j])) continue;
            if (preg_match_all($bedragRe, $regels[$j], $m)) { $v = adm_bedrag_uit(end($m[0])); if ($v !== null) return $v; }
        }
        return null;
    };
    $sleutels = [
        '/te\s*betalen|totaal\s*(te\s*betalen|incl|bedrag)|factuurbedrag|amount\s*due|total\s*(due|amount)|gesamtbetrag|rechnungsbetrag|zahlbetrag|endbetrag/i' => 3,
        '/\btotaal\b|\btotal\b|\bsumme\b/i' => 2,
    ];
    $beste = null; $besteScore = 0;
    foreach ($regels as $i => $rg) {
        foreach ($sleutels as $re => $score) {
            if (preg_match($re, $rg) && !preg_match('/excl|exkl|netto|subtota|zwischen/i', $rg)) {
                $v = $bedragOp($i);
                if ($v !== null && ($score > $besteScore || ($score === $besteScore && $v > $beste))) { $beste = $v; $besteScore = $score; }
            }
        }
    }
    if ($beste === null && $alle) { $beste = max(array_column($alle, 1)); }
    $r['bedrag'] = $beste;
    if (preg_match('/btw\s*verlegd|verlegd|reverse\s*charge|steuerschuldnerschaft|autoliquidation/i', $tekst)) { $r['verlegd'] = true; $r['btw'] = 0; }
    else {
        foreach ($regels as $i => $rg) {
            if (preg_match('/\b(btw|vat|mwst|ust|umsatzsteuer|omzetbelasting)\b/i', $rg) && !preg_match('/btw[\s-]*(nr|nummer|id)|vat\s*(no|number|id)|ust[\s-]*id/i', $rg)) {
                foreach ([$i, $i + 1] as $j) {
                    if (!isset($regels[$j]) || !preg_match_all($bedragRe, $regels[$j], $m)) continue;
                    $v = adm_bedrag_uit(end($m[0]));
                    if ($v !== null && $v > 0 && ($beste === null || $v <= (int)round($beste * 0.2))) { $r['btw'] = $v; break 2; }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    foreach ($regels as $i => $rg) {
        if (preg_match('/(factuur|invoice|rechnungs?)[\s-]*datum|factuurdatum|invoice\s*date|datum|date/i', $rg)) {
            $d = adm_datum_uit($rg . ' ' . ($regels[$i + 1] ?? ''));
            if ($d !== '') { $r['datum'] = $d; break; }
        }
    }
    if ($r['datum'] === '') { $r['datum'] = adm_datum_uit($tekst); }
    if (preg_match('/(factuur\s*(nummer|nr\.?|no\.?)|factuurnr\.?|invoice\s*(number|no\.?|nr\.?|#)|rechnungs?\s*-?\s*(nummer|nr\.?))\s*[:#.]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9\-\/.]{2,24})/i', $tekst, $m)) {
        $r['nummer'] = rtrim($m[5], '.');
    }
    return $r;
}
/* Nieuwe inkoopfactuur maken van een pdf */
function adm_ink_van_pdf($bin, $lev, $afzender, $termijn, $bron, $onderwerp = '') {
    $tekst = adm_pdf_tekst($bin);
    $v = adm_pdf_velden($tekst);
    $datum = $v['datum'] !== '' && $v['datum'] <= date('Y-m-d', strtotime('+7 days')) && $v['datum'] >= date('Y-m-d', strtotime('-3 years')) ? $v['datum'] : date('Y-m-d');
    $bedrag = (int)($v['bedrag'] ?? 0);
    $btw = $v['btw'];
    $soort = $v['verlegd'] ? 'verlegd' : 'nl';
    $controleren = $tekst === '' || $v['bedrag'] === null || $v['datum'] === '' || ($btw === null && !$v['verlegd']);
    if ($btw === null) { $btw = 0; }
    return ['id' => adm_id('i'), 'leverancier' => cut($lev, 100), 'afzender' => $afzender, 'nummer' => cut($v['nummer'], 40), 'datum' => $datum, 'termijn' => (int)$termijn,
        'verval' => date('Y-m-d', strtotime($datum . ' +' . (int)$termijn . ' days')), 'bedrag' => $bedrag, 'btw' => (int)$btw, 'btwsoort' => $soort,
        'oms' => cut($onderwerp, 140), 'status' => 'open', 'betaald_op' => '', 'pdf' => adm_pdf_bewaar($bin), 'hash' => sha1($bin), 'bron' => $bron,
        'controleren' => $controleren, 'leesbaar' => $tekst !== '', 'aangemaakt' => date('Y-m-d H:i')];
}

/* ---------- mailbox (IMAP) zonder extra PHP-onderdelen ---------- */
/* Servers van een mailbox zelf uitzoeken aan de hand van het e-mailadres */
function adm_mail_kandidaten($email) {
    $dom = strtolower(substr(strrchr($email, '@'), 1));
    $bekend = [
        'gmail.com' => ['imap.gmail.com', 'smtp.gmail.com', 465], 'googlemail.com' => ['imap.gmail.com', 'smtp.gmail.com', 465],
        'outlook.com' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587], 'hotmail.com' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587],
        'hotmail.nl' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587], 'live.nl' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587],
        'live.com' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587], 'msn.com' => ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp-mail.outlook.com', 587],
        'icloud.com' => ['imap.mail.me.com', 'smtp.mail.me.com', 587], 'me.com' => ['imap.mail.me.com', 'smtp.mail.me.com', 587],
        'yahoo.com' => ['imap.mail.yahoo.com', 'smtp.mail.yahoo.com', 465],
    ];
    if (isset($bekend[$dom])) return [$bekend[$dom]];
    $uit = [];
    $mx = [];
    if ($dom !== '' && function_exists('getmxrr')) { @getmxrr($dom, $mx); }
    $mxs = strtolower(implode(' ', $mx));
    if (strpos($mxs, 'webreus') !== false) { $uit[] = ['webreus.email', 'webreus.email', 465]; }
    if (strpos($mxs, 'google') !== false) { $uit[] = ['imap.gmail.com', 'smtp.gmail.com', 465]; }
    if (strpos($mxs, 'outlook.com') !== false) { $uit[] = ['outlook.office365.com', 'smtp.office365.com', 587]; }
    foreach (['mail.' . $dom, 'imap.' . $dom] as $h) { $uit[] = [$h, strpos($h, 'imap.') === 0 ? 'smtp.' . $dom : $h, 465]; }
    foreach ($mx as $x) {
        $d = explode('.', strtolower(rtrim($x, '.')));
        if (count($d) >= 2) { $basis = $d[count($d) - 2] . '.' . $d[count($d) - 1]; if ($basis !== $dom) { $uit[] = ['mail.' . $basis, 'mail.' . $basis, 465]; } }
    }
    $uit[] = ['webreus.email', 'webreus.email', 465];
    $gezien = []; $schoon = [];
    foreach ($uit as $u) { if (!isset($gezien[$u[0]])) { $gezien[$u[0]] = 1; $schoon[] = $u; } }
    return $schoon;
}
function adm_mail_zoek($email, $pass, &$fout) {
    @set_time_limit(180);
    $fout = ''; $pogingen = 0; $fouten = [];
    foreach (adm_mail_kandidaten($email) as $k) {
        if ($pogingen >= 5) break;
        if (filter_var(gethostbyname($k[0]), FILTER_VALIDATE_IP) === false) continue;
        $pogingen++;
        $f = '';
        $s = adm_imap_open($k[0], 993, $email, $pass, $f);
        if ($s) { adm_imap_cmd($s, 'LOGOUT'); fclose($s); return $k; }
        $fouten[] = $f;
    }
    $fout = $fouten ? end($fouten) : 'De mailserver van ' . substr(strrchr($email, '@'), 1) . ' is niet gevonden.';
    return null;
}
function adm_imap_open($host, $port, $user, $pass, &$fout) {
    $fout = '';
    if (!function_exists('stream_socket_client')) { $fout = 'Je hosting staat het ophalen van mail niet toe.'; return false; }
    $ctx = stream_context_create(['ssl' => ['verify_peer' => true, 'verify_peer_name' => true, 'SNI_enabled' => true, 'peer_name' => $host]]);
    $ssl = (int)$port === 993;
    $s = @stream_socket_client(($ssl ? 'ssl://' : 'tcp://') . $host . ':' . (int)$port, $en, $es, 15, STREAM_CLIENT_CONNECT, $ctx);
    if (!$s) {
        $laatst = (string)(error_get_last()['message'] ?? '');
        if (stripos($laatst, 'certificate') !== false || stripos($laatst, 'SSL') !== false) { $fout = 'De mailserver ' . $host . ' heeft een beveiligingscertificaat met een andere naam. Vul de servernaam in die je hosting of mailprovider opgeeft voor IMAP (die staat ook in je mailprogramma).'; }
        else { $fout = 'Geen verbinding met ' . $host . ' (poort ' . (int)$port . '). Controleer de server en de poort.'; }
        return false;
    }
    stream_set_timeout($s, 30);
    $hallo = fgets($s);
    if ($hallo === false || strpos($hallo, '* OK') !== 0) { $fout = 'De mailserver antwoordt niet zoals verwacht.'; fclose($s); return false; }
    if (!$ssl) {
        list($ok) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'STARTTLS');
        if (!$ok || !@stream_socket_enable_crypto($s, true, STREAM_CRYPTO_METHOD_TLS_CLIENT)) { $fout = 'Beveiligde verbinding lukt niet. Gebruik poort 993.'; fclose($s); return false; }
    }
    $q = function($x) { return '"' . addcslashes($x, "\"\\") . '"'; };
    list($ok, $raw) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'LOGIN ' . $q($user) . ' ' . $q($pass));
    if (!$ok) { $fout = 'Inloggen bij de mailbox lukt niet. Controleer het e-mailadres en wachtwoord.' . (stripos($host, 'gmail') !== false ? ' Bij Gmail heb je een app-wachtwoord nodig.' : ''); fclose($s); return false; }
    return $s;
}
function adm_imap_cmd($s, $cmd) {
    static $n = 0;
    $tag = 'A' . (++$n);
    fwrite($s, $tag . ' ' . $cmd . "\r\n");
    $raw = '';
    $begin = time();
    while (!feof($s)) {
        $line = fgets($s);
        if ($line === false) { $meta = stream_get_meta_data($s); if (!empty($meta['timed_out']) || time() - $begin > 60) break; continue; }
        if (preg_match('/\{(\d+)\}\r\n$/', $line, $m)) {
            $raw .= $line;
            $nodig = (int)$m[1]; $stuk = '';
            while (strlen($stuk) < $nodig && !feof($s)) { $b = fread($s, min(65536, $nodig - strlen($stuk))); if ($b === false || $b === '') { if (time() - $begin > 120) break; continue; } $stuk .= $b; }
            $raw .= $stuk;
            continue;
        }
        if (strpos($line, $tag . ' ') === 0) { return [strpos($line, $tag . ' OK') === 0, $raw, $line]; }
        $raw .= $line;
    }
    return [false, $raw, ''];
}
function adm_mime_kop($x) {
    $x = trim((string)$x);
    if ($x === '') return '';
    if (function_exists('iconv_mime_decode')) { $d = @iconv_mime_decode($x, ICONV_MIME_DECODE_CONTINUE_ON_ERROR, 'UTF-8'); if ($d !== false) return $d; }
    if (function_exists('mb_decode_mimeheader')) return mb_decode_mimeheader($x);
    return $x;
}
function adm_koppen($blok) {
    $blok = preg_replace("/\r?\n[ \t]+/", ' ', $blok);
    $k = [];
    foreach (preg_split("/\r?\n/", $blok) as $r) { if (preg_match('/^([A-Za-z0-9-]+):\s*(.*)$/', $r, $m)) { $k[strtolower($m[1])] = $m[2]; } }
    return $k;
}
function adm_van($from) {
    $from = adm_mime_kop($from);
    $email = preg_match('/<([^>]+)>/', $from, $m) ? $m[1] : (preg_match('/[^\s<>"]+@[^\s<>"]+/', $from, $m) ? $m[0] : '');
    $naam = trim(preg_replace('/<[^>]*>/', '', $from), " \"'");
    if ($naam === '' || strpos($naam, '@') !== false) { $naam = $email; }
    return [strtolower(trim($email)), $naam];
}
/* Alle pdf-bijlagen uit een complete mail halen */
function adm_mime_pdfs($raw, $diepte = 0) {
    if ($diepte > 6) return [];
    $deel = preg_split("/\r?\n\r?\n/", $raw, 2);
    $k = adm_koppen($deel[0]);
    $body = $deel[1] ?? '';
    $ct = $k['content-type'] ?? 'text/plain';
    if (stripos($ct, 'multipart/') === 0 && preg_match('/boundary="?([^";]+)"?/i', $ct, $m)) {
        $uit = [];
        $stukken = explode('--' . $m[1], $body);
        array_shift($stukken);
        foreach ($stukken as $st) {
            if (strpos($st, '--') === 0) break;
            $uit = array_merge($uit, adm_mime_pdfs(ltrim($st, "\r\n"), $diepte + 1));
        }
        return $uit;
    }
    if (stripos($ct, 'message/rfc822') === 0) return adm_mime_pdfs($body, $diepte + 1);
    $naam = '';
    foreach ([($k['content-disposition'] ?? ''), $ct] as $h) {
        if (preg_match("/filename\*=(?:UTF-8|utf-8)''([^;]+)/", $h, $m)) { $naam = rawurldecode($m[1]); break; }
        if (preg_match('/(?:file)?name="?([^";]+)"?/i', $h, $m)) { $naam = adm_mime_kop($m[1]); break; }
    }
    if (stripos($ct, 'application/pdf') === false && !preg_match('/\.pdf$/i', $naam)) return [];
    $enc = strtolower(trim($k['content-transfer-encoding'] ?? ''));
    $bin = $enc === 'base64' ? base64_decode(preg_replace('/\s+/', '', $body)) : ($enc === 'quoted-printable' ? quoted_printable_decode($body) : $body);
    return substr((string)$bin, 0, 5) === '%PDF-' ? [[$naam !== '' ? $naam : 'factuur.pdf', $bin]] : [];
}
/* Mail ophalen: pdf's van bekende afzenders worden inkoopfacturen, andere afzenders met pdf komen in de lijst "nog niet in je lijst" */
function adm_mail_ophalen(&$melding, $max = 25) {
    $m = adm_mailinst();
    $melding = '';
    if ($m['host'] === '' || $m['user'] === '' || $m['pass'] === '') { $melding = 'Koppel eerst je mailbox.'; return -1; }
    $lock = DATA_DIR . '/adm_mail.lock';
    if (file_exists($lock) && time() - filemtime($lock) < 300) { $melding = 'Mail wordt al opgehaald. Probeer het zo nog eens.'; return -1; }
    @touch($lock);
    @set_time_limit(180);
    $fout = '';
    $s = adm_imap_open($m['host'], $m['port'], $m['user'], adm_ontsleutel($m['pass']), $fout);
    if (!$s) { $m['fout'] = $fout; $m['laatst'] = date('Y-m-d H:i'); adm_save_mailinst($m); @unlink($lock); $melding = $fout; return -1; }
    list($ok, $raw) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'EXAMINE INBOX');
    if (preg_match('/UIDVALIDITY (\d+)/', $raw, $mm) && (string)$mm[1] !== (string)$m['uidvalidity']) { $m['uidvalidity'] = $mm[1]; $m['uidmax'] = 0; }
    if ((int)$m['uidmax'] === 0) { $m['kandidaten'] = []; }
    $maanden = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
    $t = strtotime($m['vanaf'] ?: date('Y') . '-01-01');
    $sinds = date('j', $t) . '-' . $maanden[(int)date('n', $t) - 1] . '-' . date('Y', $t);
    list($ok, $raw) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'UID SEARCH SINCE ' . $sinds);
    $uids = [];
    if (preg_match('/\* SEARCH([\d ]*)/', $raw, $mm)) { foreach (preg_split('/\s+/', trim($mm[1])) as $u) { if ($u !== '' && (int)$u > (int)$m['uidmax']) $uids[] = (int)$u; } }
    sort($uids);
    $bekend = [];
    foreach ($m['afzenders'] as $a) { $bekend[strtolower($a['email'])] = $a; }
    $inkoop = read_json(ADM_INKOOP, []);
    $hashes = array_flip(array_column($inkoop, 'hash'));
    $nieuw = 0; $gedaan = 0; $klaarTot = (int)$m['uidmax']; $gestopt = false;
    foreach (array_chunk($uids, 40) as $groep) {
        list($ok, $raw) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'UID FETCH ' . implode(',', $groep) . ' (UID RFC822.SIZE BODYSTRUCTURE BODY.PEEK[HEADER.FIELDS (FROM SUBJECT DATE)])');
        $info = [];
        foreach (preg_split('/^\* \d+ FETCH /m', $raw) as $st) {
            if (!preg_match('/UID (\d+)/', $st, $u)) continue;
            $kop = adm_koppen(preg_match('/\]\s*\{\d+\}\r\n(.*)$/s', $st, $h) ? $h[1] : '');
            list($email, $naam) = adm_van($kop['from'] ?? '');
            $bs = preg_match('/BODYSTRUCTURE (.*?)(?: BODY\[|$)/s', $st, $b) ? $b[1] : $st;
            $info[(int)$u[1]] = ['email' => $email, 'naam' => $naam, 'onderwerp' => cut(adm_mime_kop($kop['subject'] ?? ''), 140),
                'datum' => ($dt = strtotime($kop['date'] ?? '')) ? date('Y-m-d', $dt) : date('Y-m-d'),
                'pdf' => stripos($bs, '"pdf"') !== false || stripos($bs, '.pdf"') !== false,
                'size' => preg_match('/RFC822\.SIZE (\d+)/', $st, $z) ? (int)$z[1] : 0];
        }
        foreach ($groep as $uid) {
            $x = $info[$uid] ?? null;
            if ($x && $x['pdf'] && $x['email'] !== '') {
                if (isset($bekend[$x['email']])) {
                    if ($gedaan >= $max) { $gestopt = true; break 2; }
                    $gedaan++;
                    if ($x['size'] < 25 * 1024 * 1024) {
                        list($ok2, $mail) = adm_imap_cmd($s, 'UID FETCH ' . $uid . ' (BODY.PEEK[])');
                        $mail = preg_match('/\{\d+\}\r\n(.*)\)\r\n$/s', $mail, $mm) ? $mm[1] : $mail;
                        foreach (adm_mime_pdfs($mail) as $pdf) {
                            $hs = sha1($pdf[1]);
                            if (isset($hashes[$hs])) continue;
                            $a = $bekend[$x['email']];
                            if ($a['naam'] === '' && $x['naam'] !== '') {
                                foreach ($m['afzenders'] as $an => $av) { if ($av['email'] === $x['email']) { $m['afzenders'][$an]['naam'] = $x['naam']; } }
                                $a['naam'] = $bekend[$x['email']]['naam'] = $x['naam'];
                            }
                            $ink = adm_ink_van_pdf($pdf[1], $a['naam'] !== '' ? $a['naam'] : $x['naam'], $x['email'], (int)($a['termijn'] ?? 30), 'mail', $x['onderwerp']);
                            if ($ink['pdf'] === '') continue;
                            $inkoop[] = $ink; $hashes[$hs] = 1; $nieuw++;
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    $kd = $m['kandidaten'][$x['email']] ?? ['naam' => $x['naam'], 'onderwerp' => '', 'datum' => '', 'n' => 0];
                    $kd['n']++;
                    if ($x['datum'] >= $kd['datum']) { $kd['datum'] = $x['datum']; $kd['onderwerp'] = $x['onderwerp']; $kd['naam'] = $x['naam']; }
                    $m['kandidaten'][$x['email']] = $kd;
                }
            }
            $klaarTot = max($klaarTot, $uid);
        }
    }
    adm_imap_cmd($s, 'LOGOUT');
    fclose($s);
    $m['uidmax'] = $klaarTot;
    $m['laatst'] = date('Y-m-d H:i');
    $m['fout'] = '';
    adm_save_mailinst($m);
    if ($nieuw) { adm_save_inkoop($inkoop); }
    @unlink($lock);
    $melding = $nieuw ? $nieuw . ($nieuw === 1 ? ' nieuwe inkoopfactuur' : ' nieuwe inkoopfacturen') . ' opgehaald.' : 'Geen nieuwe inkoopfacturen.';
    if ($gestopt) { $melding .= ' Er staan er nog meer klaar: klik nog een keer op Mail ophalen.'; }
    return $nieuw;
}

/* ---------- bankbestanden inlezen ---------- */
function adm_bank_lees($naam, $bin, &$soort) {
    $soort = '';
    if (substr($bin, 0, 2) === 'PK') { $soort = 'Excel'; return adm_bank_csvrijen(adm_xlsx_rijen($bin)); }
    $t = $bin;
    if (substr($t, 0, 3) === "\xEF\xBB\xBF") { $t = substr($t, 3); }
    if (!preg_match('//u', $t)) { $t = mb_convert_encoding($t, 'UTF-8', 'Windows-1252'); }
    if (strpos($t, ':61:') !== false && (strpos($t, ':20:') !== false || strpos($t, ':25:') !== false)) { $soort = 'MT940'; return adm_mt940($t); }
    if (stripos($t, '<BkToCstmrStmt') !== false || stripos($t, '<Ntry>') !== false) { $soort = 'CAMT'; return adm_camt($t); }
    $soort = 'CSV';
    return adm_bank_csvrijen(adm_csv_rijen($t));
}
function adm_mt940($t) {
    $t = str_replace("\r\n", "\n", $t);
    $uit = [];
    preg_match_all('/^:61:(.*?)(?=^:\d{2}[A-Z]?:|\z)/ms', $t, $blokken, PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE);
    foreach ($blokken[1] as $b) {
        $regel = $b[0];
        $na = substr($t, $b[1] + strlen($regel));
        $oms = preg_match('/^:86:(.*?)(?=^:\d{2}[A-Z]?:|\z)/ms', $na, $o) && strpos(substr($na, 0, 6), ':86:') === 0 ? $o[1] : '';
        $eerste = strtok($regel, "\n");
        if (!preg_match('/^(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{4})?(R?[CD])([A-Z])?(\d+,\d{0,2})/', $eerste, $m)) continue;
        $datum = '20' . $m[1] . '-' . $m[2] . '-' . $m[3];
        $bedrag = (int)round((float)str_replace(',', '.', $m[7]) * 100);
        if ($m[5] === 'D' || $m[5] === 'RC') { $bedrag = -$bedrag; }
        $oms = trim($oms);
        $oms = preg_replace('/\s*\n\s*/', strpos($oms, '/') === 0 ? '' : ' ', $oms);
        $naam = ''; $iban = ''; $tekst = $oms;
        if (strpos($oms, '/') === 0) {
            $velden = [];
            if (preg_match_all('#/(CNTP|NAME|IBAN|REMI|EREF|ORDP|BENM|ADDR|MARF|CSID|BIC|TRCD|TRTP|BBAN|ULTB|ULTD|PURP|SVCL|RTRN)/#', $oms, $tags, PREG_OFFSET_CAPTURE)) {
                foreach ($tags[1] as $n => $tg) {
                    $beg = $tg[1] + strlen($tg[0]) + 1;
                    $eind = isset($tags[0][$n + 1]) ? $tags[0][$n + 1][1] : strlen($oms);
                    $velden[$tg[0]] = trim(substr($oms, $beg, $eind - $beg), '/ ');
                }
            }
            if (isset($velden['CNTP'])) { $c = explode('/', $velden['CNTP']); $iban = $c[0] ?? ''; $naam = $c[2] ?? ''; }
            if (isset($velden['NAME'])) { $naam = $velden['NAME']; }
            if (isset($velden['IBAN'])) { $iban = $velden['IBAN']; }
            if (isset($velden['ORDP']) && $naam === '') { $naam = preg_replace('#^.*?NAME/#', '', $velden['ORDP']); }
            if (isset($velden['BENM']) && $naam === '') { $naam = preg_replace('#^.*?NAME/#', '', $velden['BENM']); }
            $tekst = isset($velden['REMI']) ? preg_replace('#^(USTD|STRD)//?(CUR/)?#', '', $velden['REMI']) : '';
            if ($tekst === '' && isset($velden['EREF'])) { $tekst = $velden['EREF']; }
        } elseif (preg_match('/^([A-Z]{2}\d{2}[A-Z0-9]{8,30})\s+(.*)$/', $oms, $mm)) {
            $iban = $mm[1]; $tekst = $mm[2];
        }
        if ($naam === '') { $naam = cut($tekst, 40); }
        $uit[] = ['datum' => $datum, 'bedrag' => $bedrag, 'naam' => trim($naam), 'iban' => trim($iban), 'oms' => trim($tekst)];
    }
    return $uit;
}
function adm_camt($t) {
    $t = preg_replace('/\sxmlns(:\w+)?="[^"]*"/', '', $t);
    $t = preg_replace('/<(\/?)\w+:/', '<$1', $t);
    $x = @simplexml_load_string($t);
    if (!$x) return [];
    $uit = [];
    foreach ($x->xpath('//Ntry') as $n) {
        $bedrag = (int)round((float)$n->Amt * 100);
        if ((string)$n->CdtDbtInd === 'DBIT') { $bedrag = -$bedrag; }
        $datum = (string)($n->BookgDt->Dt ?? '') ?: substr((string)($n->BookgDt->DtTm ?? ''), 0, 10);
        $tx = $n->NtryDtls->TxDtls ?? null;
        $naam = ''; $iban = ''; $oms = '';
        if ($tx) {
            $partij = $bedrag < 0 ? $tx->RltdPties->Cdtr : $tx->RltdPties->Dbtr;
            $rek = $bedrag < 0 ? $tx->RltdPties->CdtrAcct : $tx->RltdPties->DbtrAcct;
            $naam = (string)($partij->Nm ?? '') ?: (string)($partij->Pty->Nm ?? '');
            $iban = (string)($rek->Id->IBAN ?? '');
            $oms = trim(implode(' ', array_map('strval', iterator_to_array($tx->RmtInf->Ustrd ?? [], false))));
            if ($oms === '') { $oms = (string)($tx->RmtInf->Strd->CdtrRefInf->Ref ?? ''); }
        }
        if ($oms === '') { $oms = (string)($n->AddtlNtryInf ?? ''); }
        if ($naam === '') { $naam = cut($oms, 40); }
        if (!preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $datum)) continue;
        $uit[] = ['datum' => $datum, 'bedrag' => $bedrag, 'naam' => trim($naam), 'iban' => trim($iban), 'oms' => trim($oms)];
    }
    return $uit;
}
function adm_csv_rijen($t) {
    $regels = preg_split("/\r\n|\n|\r/", trim($t));
    $eerste = $regels[0] ?? '';
    $sep = ';'; $max = 0;
    foreach ([';', ',', "\t", '|'] as $s) { $n = substr_count($eerste, $s); if ($n > $max) { $max = $n; $sep = $s; } }
    $rijen = [];
    $h = fopen('php://temp', 'r+'); fwrite($h, $t); rewind($h);
    while (($r = fgetcsv($h, 0, $sep, '"', '\\')) !== false) { if ($r !== [null]) { $rijen[] = $r; } }
    fclose($h);
    return $rijen;
}
function adm_xlsx_rijen($bin) {
    if (!class_exists('ZipArchive')) return [];
    $tmp = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'xl');
    file_put_contents($tmp, $bin);
    $z = new ZipArchive();
    if ($z->open($tmp) !== true) { @unlink($tmp); return []; }
    $gedeeld = [];
    if (($ss = $z->getFromName('xl/sharedStrings.xml')) !== false) {
        $x = @simplexml_load_string($ss);
        if ($x) { foreach ($x->si as $si) { $gedeeld[] = isset($si->t) ? (string)$si->t : implode('', array_map('strval', iterator_to_array($si->r ? $si->xpath('.//*[local-name()="t"]') : [], false))); } }
    }
    $blad = $z->getFromName('xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml');
    $z->close(); @unlink($tmp);
    if ($blad === false) return [];
    $x = @simplexml_load_string($blad);
    if (!$x) return [];
    $rijen = [];
    foreach ($x->sheetData->row as $row) {
        $r = [];
        foreach ($row->c as $c) {
            $ref = (string)$c['r'];
            $kol = 0;
            foreach (str_split(preg_replace('/\d+/', '', $ref)) as $l) { $kol = $kol * 26 + (ord($l) - 64); }
            $v = (string)$c->v;
            if ((string)$c['t'] === 's') { $v = $gedeeld[(int)$v] ?? ''; }
            elseif ((string)$c['t'] === 'inlineStr') { $v = (string)$c->is->t; }
            $r[$kol - 1] = $v;
        }
        if ($r) { $m = max(array_keys($r)); $vol = []; for ($i = 0; $i <= $m; $i++) { $vol[] = $r[$i] ?? ''; } $rijen[] = $vol; }
    }
    return $rijen;
}
function adm_csv_datum($v) {
    $v = trim((string)$v);
    if (preg_match('/^\d{5}(\.\d+)?$/', $v)) return date('Y-m-d', ((int)$v - 25569) * 86400);
    if (preg_match('/^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})$/', $v, $m)) return $m[1] . '-' . $m[2] . '-' . $m[3];
    if (preg_match('/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/', $v, $m)) return $m[1] . '-' . $m[2] . '-' . $m[3];
    if (preg_match('/^(\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{4})/', $v, $m)) return sprintf('%04d-%02d-%02d', $m[3], $m[2], $m[1]);
    return '';
}
function adm_csv_bedrag($v) {
    $v = str_replace(["\u{20ac}", ' ', "\xC2\xA0", 'EUR'], '', trim((string)$v));
    if ($v === '') return null;
    $neg = false;
    if ($v[0] === '+') { $v = substr($v, 1); }
    if ($v[0] === '-') { $neg = true; $v = substr($v, 1); }
    if (preg_match('/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/', $v) && substr_count($v, '.') === 1 && strlen(substr($v, strpos($v, '.') + 1)) !== 3) { $n = (float)$v; }
    elseif (strpos($v, ',') !== false) { $n = (float)str_replace(',', '.', str_replace('.', '', $v)); }
    elseif (is_numeric($v)) { $n = (float)$v; }
    else return null;
    $c = (int)round($n * 100);
    return $neg ? -$c : $c;
}
/* Kolommen herkennen aan de kopregel (ING, Rabobank, ABN AMRO, Knab, bunq, ASN, SNS, Triodos en meer) */
function adm_bank_csvrijen($rijen) {
    if (!$rijen) return [];
    $kop = null; $start = 0;
    foreach (array_slice($rijen, 0, 8) as $i => $r) {
        $l = strtolower(implode('|', $r));
        if ((strpos($l, 'datum') !== false || strpos($l, 'date') !== false) && (strpos($l, 'bedrag') !== false || strpos($l, 'amount') !== false)) { $kop = array_map(function($x) { return strtolower(trim((string)$x)); }, $r); $start = $i + 1; break; }
    }
    $uit = [];
    if ($kop === null) {
        /* ABN AMRO tab-bestand zonder kopregel: rekening, munt, datum, saldo, saldo, rentedatum, bedrag, omschrijving */
        foreach ($rijen as $r) {
            if (count($r) >= 8 && preg_match('/^\d{8}$/', trim($r[2])) && adm_csv_bedrag($r[6]) !== null) {
                $oms = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $r[7]));
                $naam = preg_match('/Naam:\s*(.*?)\s+(Omschrijving|Kenmerk|IBAN|Machtiging):/i', $oms, $m) ? $m[1] : cut($oms, 40);
                $iban = preg_match('/IBAN:\s*([A-Z]{2}\d{2}[A-Z0-9]+)/', $oms, $m) ? $m[1] : '';
                $tekst = preg_match('/Omschrijving:\s*(.*?)(\s+(Kenmerk|IBAN|Machtiging):|$)/i', $oms, $m) ? $m[1] : $oms;
                $uit[] = ['datum' => adm_csv_datum($r[2]), 'bedrag' => adm_csv_bedrag($r[6]), 'naam' => $naam, 'iban' => $iban, 'oms' => $tekst];
            }
        }
        return array_values(array_filter($uit, function($x) { return $x['datum'] !== ''; }));
    }
    $zoek = function($opties) use ($kop) {
        foreach ($opties as $o) { foreach ($kop as $i => $k) { if ($k === $o) return $i; } }
        foreach ($opties as $o) { foreach ($kop as $i => $k) { if ($o !== '' && strpos($k, $o) === 0) return $i; } }
        return null;
    };
    $kDatum = $zoek(['transactiedatum', 'boekingsdatum', 'datum', 'date', 'transactiondate', 'boekdatum']);
    $kBedrag = $zoek(['bedrag (eur)', 'bedrag', 'transactiebedrag', 'amount']);
    $kAfBij = $zoek(['af bij', 'af/bij', 'debet/credit', 'credit/debet', 'debit/credit', 'creditdebet', 'debetcredit', 'credit debet', 'debet credit', 'bij/af']);
    $kNaam = $zoek(['naam tegenpartij', 'naam / omschrijving', 'naam/omschrijving', 'tegenrekeninghouder', 'naam tegenrekening', 'naam begunstigde', 'tegenpartij', 'name', 'naam']);
    $kIban = $zoek(['tegenrekening iban/bban', 'tegenrekening', 'iban tegenpartij', 'tegenrekeningnummer', 'counterparty iban', 'rekening tegenpartij']);
    $kOms = [];
    foreach ($kop as $i => $k) { if (preg_match('/^(omschrijving|mededelingen|description|betalingskenmerk|omschrijving-\d)/', $k) && $i !== $kNaam) { $kOms[] = $i; } }
    if ($kDatum === null || $kBedrag === null) return [];
    foreach (array_slice($rijen, $start) as $r) {
        $datum = adm_csv_datum($r[$kDatum] ?? '');
        $bedrag = adm_csv_bedrag($r[$kBedrag] ?? '');
        if ($datum === '' || $bedrag === null) continue;
        if ($kAfBij !== null) {
            $ab = strtolower(trim($r[$kAfBij] ?? ''));
            if (in_array($ab, ['af', 'd', 'debet', 'debit'], true)) { $bedrag = -abs($bedrag); }
            elseif (in_array($ab, ['bij', 'c', 'credit'], true)) { $bedrag = abs($bedrag); }
        }
        $oms = trim(implode(' ', array_filter(array_map(function($i) use ($r) { return trim($r[$i] ?? ''); }, $kOms))));
        $naam = $kNaam !== null ? trim($r[$kNaam] ?? '') : '';
        if ($naam === '') { $naam = cut($oms, 40); }
        $uit[] = ['datum' => $datum, 'bedrag' => $bedrag, 'naam' => $naam, 'iban' => $kIban !== null ? trim($r[$kIban] ?? '') : '', 'oms' => preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', $oms)];
    }
    return $uit;
}
function adm_bank_sleutel($naam) { return trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', strtolower((string)$naam))); }
/* Voor het onthouden van keuzes: winkelnummers en tekens tellen niet mee (Albert Heijn 1234 = Albert Heijn 5678) */
function adm_regel_sleutel($naam, $bedrag = 0) {
    $n = mb_strtolower((string)$naam);
    $n = preg_replace('/^(ccv|bck|sumup|zettle|iz|sq|pay\.nl)\s*\*\s*/', '', $n);
    $n = trim(preg_replace('/\s+/', ' ', preg_replace('/[^a-z ]+/', ' ', preg_replace('/\d{2,}/', ' ', $n))));
    return $n === '' ? '' : ($bedrag < 0 ? 'af:' : 'bij:') . $n;
}
/* Betalingen koppelen aan open facturen en indelen volgens onthouden keuzes */
function adm_bank_koppel(&$bank, &$docs, &$inkoop) {
    $regels = read_json(ADM_BANKREGELS, []);
    $n = 0;
    foreach ($bank as $bi => $t) {
        if ($t['indeling'] !== '' || $t['koppel'] !== '') continue;
        $tekst = strtolower($t['naam'] . ' ' . $t['oms']);
        if ($t['bedrag'] > 0) {
            $kand = [];
            foreach ($docs as $di => $d) {
                if ($d['soort'] !== 'factuur' || $d['status'] !== 'open' || !empty($d['credit'])) continue;
                if (adm_tot($d)['incl'] !== $t['bedrag']) continue;
                $score = 1;
                if ($d['nummer'] !== '' && strpos($tekst, strtolower($d['nummer'])) !== false) $score += 5;
                if (adm_lijkt($d['klant']['naam'], $t['naam'])) $score += 3;
                $kand[] = [$score, $di];
            }
            rsort($kand);
            if ($kand && ($kand[0][0] > 1 || count($kand) === 1)) {
                $di = $kand[0][1];
                $docs[$di]['status'] = 'betaald'; $docs[$di]['betaald_op'] = $t['datum'];
                $bank[$bi]['koppel'] = 'd:' . $docs[$di]['id']; $bank[$bi]['indeling'] = 'omzet'; $n++;
                continue;
            }
        } else {
            $kand = [];
            foreach ($inkoop as $ii => $x) {
                if ($x['status'] !== 'open' || $x['bedrag'] !== -$t['bedrag']) continue;
                $score = 1;
                if ($x['nummer'] !== '' && strpos($tekst, strtolower($x['nummer'])) !== false) $score += 5;
                if (adm_lijkt($x['leverancier'], $t['naam'])) $score += 3;
                $kand[] = [$score, $ii];
            }
            rsort($kand);
            if ($kand && ($kand[0][0] > 1 || count($kand) === 1)) {
                $ii = $kand[0][1];
                $inkoop[$ii]['status'] = 'betaald'; $inkoop[$ii]['betaald_op'] = $t['datum'];
                $bank[$bi]['koppel'] = 'i:' . $inkoop[$ii]['id']; $bank[$bi]['indeling'] = 'zakelijk'; $n++;
                continue;
            }
        }
        $k = adm_regel_sleutel($t['naam'], $t['bedrag']);
        if ($k !== '' && isset($regels[$k])) {
            $bank[$bi]['indeling'] = $regels[$k]['indeling'];
            $bank[$bi]['btw'] = (int)($regels[$k]['btw'] ?? 21);
            $n++;
        }
    }
    return $n;
}
function adm_lijkt($a, $b) {
    $a = preg_replace('/\b(b\.?v\.?|v\.?o\.?f\.?|gmbh|bv|nv|vof)\b|[^a-z0-9 ]/', '', strtolower($a));
    $b = preg_replace('/\b(b\.?v\.?|v\.?o\.?f\.?|gmbh|bv|nv|vof)\b|[^a-z0-9 ]/', '', strtolower($b));
    $a = trim($a); $b = trim($b);
    if ($a === '' || $b === '') return false;
    $wa = explode(' ', $a);
    foreach ($wa as $w) { if (strlen($w) >= 4 && strpos($b, $w) !== false) return true; }
    return false;
}

/* ---------- acties: inkoop, mailbox, bank en losse verkoopfacturen ---------- */
function adm_upload_pdf($veld, &$fout) {
    $fout = '';
    if (empty($_FILES[$veld]) || ($_FILES[$veld]['error'] ?? 4) === 4) return null;
    if ($_FILES[$veld]['error'] !== 0) { $fout = 'Het bestand kwam niet goed binnen. Is het groter dan ' . ini_get('upload_max_filesize') . '?'; return null; }
    $bin = (string)file_get_contents($_FILES[$veld]['tmp_name']);
    if (substr($bin, 0, 5) !== '%PDF-') { $fout = 'Dit is geen pdf-bestand.'; return null; }
    return $bin;
}
function adm_ajax() { return ($_POST['ajax'] ?? '') === '1'; }
function adm_acties2($action) {
    $inkoop = adm_inkoop();
    $id = (string)($_POST['id'] ?? '');

    /* inkoop */
    if ($action === 'adm_ink_save') {
        $i = $id !== '' ? adm_vind($inkoop, $id) : null;
        $fout = '';
        $bin = adm_upload_pdf('pdf', $fout);
        if ($fout !== '') { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: ' . $fout); }
        if ($i === null) {
            if ($bin !== null) { $x = adm_ink_van_pdf($bin, '', '', 30, 'hand'); $x['controleren'] = false; }
            else { $x = ['id' => adm_id('i'), 'afzender' => '', 'pdf' => '', 'hash' => '', 'bron' => 'hand', 'status' => 'open', 'betaald_op' => '', 'controleren' => false, 'aangemaakt' => date('Y-m-d H:i')]; }
            $lege = trim((string)($_POST['bedrag'] ?? '')) === '' && $bin !== null;
        } else { $x = $inkoop[$i]; $lege = false; }
        if ($bin !== null && $i !== null) { if ($x['pdf'] !== '' && adm_pdf_pad($x['pdf'])) { @unlink(adm_pdf_pad($x['pdf'])); } $x['pdf'] = adm_pdf_bewaar($bin); $x['hash'] = sha1($bin); }
        $x['leverancier'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['leverancier'] ?? '')), 100);
        if ($x['leverancier'] === '') { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: vul de leverancier in.'); }
        if (!$lege) {
            $x['nummer'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['nummer'] ?? '')), 40);
            $d = (string)($_POST['datum'] ?? ''); $x['datum'] = preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $d) ? $d : date('Y-m-d');
            $x['bedrag'] = adm_cent($_POST['bedrag'] ?? '');
            $x['btwsoort'] = in_array($_POST['btwsoort'] ?? '', ['nl', 'verlegd', 'geen'], true) ? $_POST['btwsoort'] : 'nl';
            $x['btw'] = $x['btwsoort'] === 'nl' ? adm_cent($_POST['btw'] ?? '') : 0;
            $x['controleren'] = false;
        } else { $x['controleren'] = true; }
        $x['termijn'] = max(0, min(180, (int)($_POST['termijn'] ?? 30)));
        $x['verval'] = date('Y-m-d', strtotime($x['datum'] . ' +' . $x['termijn'] . ' days'));
        $x['oms'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['oms'] ?? ($x['oms'] ?? ''))), 140);
        if ($i !== null) { $inkoop[$i] = $x; } else { $inkoop[] = $x; }
        adm_save_inkoop($inkoop);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=ink&id=' . rawurlencode($x['id']), $lege ? 'De pdf is ingelezen. Controleer de gegevens en sla op.' : 'Inkoopfactuur van ' . $x['leverancier'] . ' is opgeslagen.');
    }
    if (in_array($action, ['adm_ink_status', 'adm_ink_del'], true)) {
        $i = adm_vind($inkoop, $id);
        if ($i === null) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=inkoop', 'Deze inkoopfactuur bestaat niet meer.'); }
        if ($action === 'adm_ink_del') {
            if ($inkoop[$i]['pdf'] !== '' && adm_pdf_pad($inkoop[$i]['pdf'])) { @unlink(adm_pdf_pad($inkoop[$i]['pdf'])); }
            $lev = $inkoop[$i]['leverancier'];
            array_splice($inkoop, $i, 1); adm_save_inkoop($inkoop);
            adm_terug('admin.php?adm=inkoop', 'Inkoopfactuur van ' . $lev . ' is verwijderd.');
        }
        $st = (string)($_POST['status'] ?? '');
        if (!in_array($st, ['open', 'betaald', 'genegeerd'], true)) { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Deze status kan niet.'); }
        $inkoop[$i]['status'] = $st;
        $bo = (string)($_POST['betaald_op'] ?? '');
        $inkoop[$i]['betaald_op'] = $st === 'betaald' ? (preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $bo) ? $bo : date('Y-m-d')) : '';
        if ($st !== 'open') { $inkoop[$i]['controleren'] = false; }
        adm_save_inkoop($inkoop);
        adm_terug(adm_ref(), $inkoop[$i]['leverancier'] . ': ' . ['open' => 'staat weer open', 'betaald' => 'betaald', 'genegeerd' => 'genegeerd. Hij telt niet meer mee.'][$st]);
    }

    /* mailbox */
    $m = adm_mailinst();
    if ($action === 'adm_mail_inst') {
        $oudUser = $m['user']; $oudHost = $m['host']; $oudSmtp = $m['smtp_host'];
        $m['host'] = cut(strtolower(trim((string)($_POST['host'] ?? ''))), 120);
        $m['port'] = in_array((int)($_POST['port'] ?? 993), [993, 143], true) ? (int)($_POST['port'] ?? 993) : 993;
        $m['user'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['user'] ?? '')), 120);
        $m['smtp_host'] = cut(strtolower(trim((string)($_POST['smtp_host'] ?? ''))), 120);
        $m['smtp_port'] = in_array((int)($_POST['smtp_port'] ?? 465), [465, 587], true) ? (int)($_POST['smtp_port'] ?? 465) : 465;
        $m['smtp_aan'] = !empty($_POST['smtp_aan']);
        if ((string)($_POST['pass'] ?? '') !== '') { $m['pass'] = adm_versleutel((string)$_POST['pass']); }
        /* Ander e-mailadres en de servers niet aangepast: dan de servers opnieuw zelf uitzoeken */
        if (strcasecmp($oudUser, $m['user']) !== 0 && $m['host'] === $oudHost && $m['smtp_host'] === $oudSmtp) { $m['host'] = ''; $m['smtp_host'] = ''; }
        $v = (string)($_POST['vanaf'] ?? '');
        if (preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $v) && $v !== $m['vanaf']) { $m['vanaf'] = $v; $m['uidmax'] = 0; }
        $naDoc = preg_match('/^[a-z0-9]{4,30}$/', (string)($_POST['na_doc'] ?? '')) ? (string)$_POST['na_doc'] : '';
        $terugNa = $naDoc !== '' ? 'admin.php?adm=mail&id=' . $naDoc : 'admin.php?adm=mailbox';
        $terugFout = 'admin.php?adm=mailbox&zelf=1' . ($naDoc !== '' ? '&na=' . $naDoc : '');
        if (!filter_var($m['user'], FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) { adm_save_mailinst($m); adm_terug($terugFout, 'Vul je volledige e-mailadres in, bijvoorbeeld info@jouwbedrijf.nl.'); }
        if ($m['pass'] === '') { adm_save_mailinst($m); adm_terug($terugFout, 'Vul het wachtwoord van je e-mail in.'); }
        $fout = '';
        if ($m['host'] === '') {
            $k = adm_mail_zoek($m['user'], adm_ontsleutel($m['pass']), $fout);
            if (!$k) { adm_save_mailinst($m); adm_terug($terugFout, 'Koppelen lukte niet: ' . $fout . ' Lukt het daarna nog steeds niet, vul dan hieronder de servers zelf in; die staan bij je mailprovider.'); }
            $m['host'] = $k[0]; $m['port'] = 993;
            if ($m['smtp_host'] === '') { $m['smtp_host'] = $k[1]; $m['smtp_port'] = $k[2]; }
        } else {
            $s = adm_imap_open($m['host'], $m['port'], $m['user'], adm_ontsleutel($m['pass']), $fout);
            if (!$s) { adm_save_mailinst($m); adm_terug($terugFout, 'Koppelen lukte niet: ' . $fout); }
            adm_imap_cmd($s, 'LOGOUT'); fclose($s);
        }
        $m['fout'] = '';
        $sf = '';
        if (!empty($m['smtp_aan'])) {
            $sf = adm_smtp_test($m);
            if ($sf !== '' && (int)$m['smtp_port'] === 465) { $m2 = $m; $m2['smtp_port'] = 587; if (adm_smtp_test($m2) === '') { $m = $m2; $sf = ''; } }
            if ($sf !== '' && $m['smtp_host'] !== $m['host']) { $m2 = $m; $m2['smtp_host'] = $m['host']; $m2['smtp_port'] = 465; if (adm_smtp_test($m2) === '') { $m = $m2; $sf = ''; } }
        }
        adm_save_mailinst($m);
        if ($sf !== '') { adm_terug($terugFout, 'Je e-mail is gekoppeld voor het ophalen van inkoopfacturen, maar versturen lukt nog niet: ' . $sf . ' Vul hieronder de uitgaande mailserver (SMTP) van je mailprovider in.'); }
        adm_terug($terugNa, 'Je e-mail is gekoppeld. Offertes en facturen gaan nu vanaf ' . $m['user'] . ' de deur uit.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_mail_los') {
        $m['pass'] = ''; $m['uidmax'] = 0; adm_save_mailinst($m);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', 'De koppeling met je mailbox is verbroken. Het wachtwoord is gewist.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_afz_add') {
        $email = strtolower(trim((string)($_POST['email'] ?? '')));
        if (!filter_var($email, FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', 'Dit e-mailadres klopt niet.'); }
        foreach ($m['afzenders'] as $a) { if ($a['email'] === $email) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', 'Deze afzender staat al in je lijst.'); } }
        $naam = cut(trim((string)($_POST['naam'] ?? '')), 100);
        if ($naam === '') { $naam = $m['kandidaten'][$email]['naam'] ?? ''; }
        $m['afzenders'][] = ['email' => $email, 'naam' => $naam, 'termijn' => max(0, min(180, (int)($_POST['termijn'] ?? 30)))];
        unset($m['kandidaten'][$email]);
        $m['uidmax'] = 0;
        adm_save_mailinst($m);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', ($naam !== '' ? $naam : $email) . ' is toegevoegd. Bij de volgende keer mail ophalen komen ook de oudere facturen van deze afzender binnen.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_afz_save') {
        foreach ($m['afzenders'] as $n => $a) {
            $k = md5($a['email']);
            if (isset($_POST['naam'][$k])) { $m['afzenders'][$n]['naam'] = cut(trim((string)$_POST['naam'][$k]), 100); }
            if (isset($_POST['termijn'][$k])) { $m['afzenders'][$n]['termijn'] = max(0, min(180, (int)$_POST['termijn'][$k])); }
        }
        adm_save_mailinst($m);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', 'De afzenders zijn opgeslagen.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_afz_del') {
        $email = (string)($_POST['email'] ?? '');
        $m['afzenders'] = array_values(array_filter($m['afzenders'], function($a) use ($email) { return $a['email'] !== $email; }));
        adm_save_mailinst($m);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=mailbox', 'De afzender is weggehaald. Facturen die al binnen zijn, blijven staan.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_mail_halen') {
        $melding = '';
        adm_mail_ophalen($melding);
        adm_terug(adm_ref(), $melding);
    }

    /* bank */
    if ($action === 'adm_bank_upload') {
        if (empty($_FILES['bestand']) || $_FILES['bestand']['error'] !== 0) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=bank', 'Kies eerst een bankbestand.'); }
        $soort = '';
        $ruw = (string)file_get_contents($_FILES['bestand']['tmp_name']);
        $nieuwe = adm_bank_lees($_FILES['bestand']['name'], $ruw, $soort);
        if (!$nieuwe) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=bank', 'In dit bestand zijn geen betalingen gevonden. Download bij je bank een MT940-, CAMT- of CSV-bestand en probeer het opnieuw.'); }
        /* het originele bestand bewaren voor het pakket van de boekhouder (dubbel = een keer) */
        ensure_data_dir(); if (!is_dir(ADM_BANKBEST_DIR)) { mkdir(ADM_BANKBEST_DIR, 0755, true); }
        $bl = read_json(ADM_BANKBEST_DIR . '/lijst.json', []); $hs = sha1($ruw);
        if (!in_array($hs, array_column($bl, 'hash'), true)) {
            $ext = strtolower(preg_replace('/[^a-z0-9]/i', '', pathinfo((string)$_FILES['bestand']['name'], PATHINFO_EXTENSION))) ?: 'txt';
            $bn = date('Ymd-His') . '-' . bin2hex(random_bytes(4)) . '.' . $ext;
            file_put_contents(ADM_BANKBEST_DIR . '/' . $bn, $ruw);
            $dts = array_column($nieuwe, 'datum'); sort($dts);
            $bl[] = ['bestand' => $bn, 'naam' => cut((string)$_FILES['bestand']['name'], 100) ?: $bn, 'van' => $dts[0], 'tot' => end($dts), 'hash' => $hs, 'op' => date('Y-m-d H:i')];
            write_json(ADM_BANKBEST_DIR . '/lijst.json', $bl);
        }
        $bank = adm_bank();
        $ids = array_flip(array_column($bank, 'id'));
        $teller = []; $erbij = 0;
        foreach ($nieuwe as $t) {
            $basis = $t['datum'] . '|' . $t['bedrag'] . '|' . adm_bank_sleutel($t['naam']) . '|' . adm_bank_sleutel($t['oms']);
            $teller[$basis] = ($teller[$basis] ?? 0) + 1;
            $tid = 't' . substr(sha1($basis . '|' . $teller[$basis]), 0, 16);
            if (isset($ids[$tid])) continue;
            $bank[] = ['id' => $tid, 'datum' => $t['datum'], 'bedrag' => $t['bedrag'], 'naam' => cut($t['naam'], 100), 'iban' => cut($t['iban'], 40), 'oms' => cut($t['oms'], 300), 'indeling' => '', 'btw' => 21, 'koppel' => ''];
            $ids[$tid] = 1; $erbij++;
        }
        $docs = adm_docs();
        $gekoppeld = adm_bank_koppel($bank, $docs, $inkoop);
        adm_save_bank($bank); adm_save_docs($docs); adm_save_inkoop($inkoop);
        $open = count(array_filter($bank, function($t) { return $t['indeling'] === ''; }));
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=bank', $soort . '-bestand ingelezen: ' . $erbij . ' nieuwe ' . ($erbij === 1 ? 'betaling' : 'betalingen') . ' (' . (count($nieuwe) - $erbij) . ' stonden er al in). ' . $gekoppeld . ' vanzelf ingedeeld of gekoppeld. Nog in te delen: ' . $open . '.');
    }
    if ($action === 'adm_bank_indeel') {
        global $ADM_INDELING;
        $bank = adm_bank();
        $i = adm_vind($bank, $id);
        if ($i === null) { adm_terug('admin.php?adm=bank', 'Deze betaling bestaat niet meer.'); }
        $ind = (string)($_POST['indeling'] ?? '');
        if (!isset($ADM_INDELING[$ind])) { $ind = ''; }
        $btw = in_array((int)($_POST['btw'] ?? 21), [21, 9, 0], true) ? (int)$_POST['btw'] : 21;
        $bank[$i]['indeling'] = $ind; $bank[$i]['btw'] = $btw;
        $aantal = 0;
        if (!empty($_POST['onthoud']) && ($k = adm_regel_sleutel($bank[$i]['naam'], $bank[$i]['bedrag'])) !== '') {
            $regels = read_json(ADM_BANKREGELS, []);
            if ($ind === '') { unset($regels[$k]); } else { $regels[$k] = ['indeling' => $ind, 'btw' => $btw]; }
            write_json(ADM_BANKREGELS, $regels);
            if ($ind !== '') {
                foreach ($bank as $j => $t) { if ($j !== $i && $t['indeling'] === '' && $t['koppel'] === '' && adm_regel_sleutel($t['naam'], $t['bedrag']) === $k) { $bank[$j]['indeling'] = $ind; $bank[$j]['btw'] = $btw; $aantal++; } }
            }
        }
        adm_save_bank($bank);
        if (adm_ajax()) { header('Content-Type: application/json'); echo json_encode(['ok' => true, 'ook' => $aantal]); exit; }
        adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Opgeslagen' . ($aantal ? '. Ook ' . $aantal . ' andere betalingen van ' . $bank[$i]['naam'] . ' zijn zo ingedeeld.' : '.'));
    }

    /* verkoopfactuur die ergens anders is gemaakt (pdf) */
    if ($action === 'adm_extern_save') {
        $docs = adm_docs(); $klanten = adm_klanten(); $inst = adm_inst();
        $i = $id !== '' ? adm_vind($docs, $id) : null;
        $fout = '';
        $bin = adm_upload_pdf('pdf', $fout);
        if ($fout !== '') { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: ' . $fout); }
        $d = $i !== null ? $docs[$i] : ['id' => adm_id('d'), 'soort' => 'factuur', 'extern' => true, 'status' => 'open', 'credit' => false, 'van' => '', 'token' => bin2hex(random_bytes(12)),
            'betaald_op' => '', 'verstuurd_op' => '', 'akkoord_op' => '', 'aangemaakt' => date('Y-m-d H:i'), 'regels' => [], 'opmerking' => '', 'leverdatum' => '', 'geldig_tot' => '', 'kor' => false, 'pdf' => ''];
        $kid = (string)($_POST['klant_id'] ?? '');
        if ($kid === 'nieuw') {
            $k = adm_klant_uit_post('nk_', ['id' => adm_id('k')]);
            if ($k['naam'] === '') { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: vul de naam van de klant in.'); }
            $klanten[] = $k; usort($klanten, function($a, $b) { return strcasecmp($a['naam'], $b['naam']); }); adm_save_klanten($klanten);
        } else {
            $ki = adm_vind($klanten, $kid);
            if ($ki === null && $i === null) { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: kies een klant.'); }
            $k = $ki !== null ? $klanten[$ki] : array_merge(['id' => $d['klant_id']], $d['klant']);
        }
        $d['klant_id'] = $k['id']; $d['klant'] = adm_snap($k);
        $d['nummer'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['nummer'] ?? '')), 40);
        if ($d['nummer'] === '') { adm_terug(adm_ref(), 'Niet opgeslagen: vul het factuurnummer in.'); }
        $dt = (string)($_POST['datum'] ?? ''); $d['datum'] = preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $dt) ? $dt : date('Y-m-d');
        $d['termijn'] = max(0, min(120, (int)($_POST['termijn'] ?? $inst['termijn'])));
        $d['onderwerp'] = cut(trim((string)($_POST['onderwerp'] ?? '')), 140);
        $pct = in_array((int)($_POST['pct'] ?? 21), [21, 9, 0], true) ? (int)$_POST['pct'] : 21;
        $incl = adm_cent($_POST['incl'] ?? '');
        $excl = (int)round($incl * 100 / (100 + $pct));
        $d['vast'] = ['excl' => $excl, 'btw' => $incl - $excl, 'pct' => $pct];
        if ($bin !== null) { if (!empty($d['pdf']) && adm_pdf_pad($d['pdf'])) { @unlink(adm_pdf_pad($d['pdf'])); } $d['pdf'] = adm_pdf_bewaar($bin); }
        if ($i !== null) { $docs[$i] = $d; } else { array_unshift($docs, $d); }
        adm_save_docs($docs);
        adm_terug('admin.php?adm=doc&id=' . rawurlencode($d['id']), 'Verkoopfactuur ' . $d['nummer'] . ' staat in je administratie.');
    }
    adm_terug('admin.php?adm', 'Deze actie kan hier niet.');
}
/* Pdf tonen (alleen ingelogd) */
function adm_pdf_toon() {
    $id = (string)($_GET['id'] ?? '');
    $naam = '';
    foreach (adm_inkoop() as $x) { if ($x['id'] === $id) { $naam = $x['pdf']; break; } }
    if ($naam === '') { foreach (adm_docs() as $x) { if ($x['id'] === $id) { $naam = $x['pdf'] ?? ''; break; } } }
    $pad = adm_pdf_pad($naam);
    if (($pad === '' || !is_file($pad)) && adm_voorbeeld()) {
        foreach (adm_inkoop() as $x) { if ($x['id'] === $id) { $pdf = adm_demo_inkpdf($x); header('Content-Type: application/pdf'); header('Content-Disposition: inline; filename="factuur.pdf"'); echo $pdf; exit; } }
    }
    if ($pad === '' || !is_file($pad)) { http_response_code(404); exit('Geen pdf gevonden.'); }
    header('Content-Type: application/pdf');
    header('Content-Disposition: inline; filename="factuur.pdf"');
    header('Content-Length: ' . filesize($pad));
    header('X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff');
    readfile($pad); exit;
}
/* Mail ophalen op de achtergrond, als de admin open staat (hooguit 1 keer per half uur) */
function adm_mail_achtergrond() {
    global $is_demo;
    header('Content-Type: application/json');
    if (!empty($is_demo)) { echo json_encode(['nieuw' => 0]); exit; }
    if (function_exists('session_write_close')) { session_write_close(); }
    $mb = adm_mailinst();
    if ($mb['host'] !== '' && $mb['pass'] !== '' && adm_ml()['check'] < date('Y-m-d H:i', strtotime('-20 minutes'))) { if (function_exists('ignore_user_abort')) { ignore_user_abort(true); } adm_ml_controleer(); }
    $ts = adm_taak_staat();
    if (substr($ts['laatst'], 0, 10) !== date('Y-m-d')) {
        $voor = count(read_json(ADM_INKOOP, []));
        adm_taak_draai('admin');
        echo json_encode(['nieuw' => max(0, count(read_json(ADM_INKOOP, [])) - $voor)]); exit;
    }
    $m = adm_mailinst();
    if ($m['host'] === '' || $m['pass'] === '' || !$m['afzenders'] || ($m['laatst'] !== '' && strtotime($m['laatst']) > time() - 1800)) { echo json_encode(['nieuw' => 0]); exit; }
    $melding = '';
    $n = adm_mail_ophalen($melding, 15);
    echo json_encode(['nieuw' => max(0, $n), 'melding' => $melding]); exit;
}
function adm_export_inkoop() {
    $jaar = preg_match('/^\d{4}$/', (string)($_GET['jaar'] ?? '')) ? $_GET['jaar'] : date('Y');
    header('Content-Type: text/csv; charset=UTF-8');
    header('Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="inkoop-' . $jaar . '.csv"');
    $out = fopen('php://output', 'w');
    fwrite($out, "\xEF\xBB\xBF");
    $f = function($c) { return number_format($c / 100, 2, ',', ''); };
    fputcsv($out, ['Datum', 'Leverancier', 'Factuurnummer', 'Omschrijving', 'Bedrag incl. btw', 'Btw', 'Btw-soort', 'Vervaldatum', 'Status', 'Betaald op'], ';');
    foreach (array_reverse(adm_inkoop()) as $x) {
        if (substr($x['datum'], 0, 4) !== (string)$jaar || $x['status'] === 'genegeerd') continue;
        fputcsv($out, [$x['datum'], $x['leverancier'], $x['nummer'], $x['oms'], $f($x['bedrag']), $f($x['btw']), ['nl' => 'Nederlandse btw', 'verlegd' => 'Btw verlegd', 'geen' => 'Geen btw'][$x['btwsoort']] ?? '', $x['verval'], adm_ink_status($x)[1], $x['betaald_op']], ';');
    }
    fclose($out);
}

/* ---------- schermen: inkoop ---------- */
function adm_v_inkoop() {
    $lijst = adm_inkoop(); $m = adm_mailinst();
    $f = (string)($_GET['f'] ?? ''); $q = (string)($_GET['k'] ?? '');
    $laat = 0; $laatN = 0; $open = 0; $openN = 0; $contr = 0;
    foreach ($lijst as $x) {
        $s = adm_ink_status($x)[0];
        if ($s === 'telaat') { $laat += $x['bedrag']; $laatN++; }
        if ($s === 'open' || $s === 'telaat') { $open += $x['bedrag']; $openN++; }
        if (!empty($x['controleren']) && $x['status'] === 'open') $contr++;
    }
    $kwartalen = [];
    foreach ($lijst as $x) { $kwartalen[substr($x['datum'], 0, 4) . '-' . (int)ceil((int)substr($x['datum'], 5, 2) / 3)] = 1; }
    krsort($kwartalen);
    $gekoppeld = $m['host'] !== '' && $m['pass'] !== '';
    ?>
    <div class="adm-stats">
      <a class="adm-stat<?= $laatN ? ' rood' : '' ?>" href="admin.php?adm=inkoop&amp;f=telaat"><small>Inkoop te laat</small><strong><?= h(adm_eur($laat)) ?></strong><span><?= $laatN ?> <?= $laatN === 1 ? 'factuur' : 'facturen' ?></span></a>
      <a class="adm-stat" href="admin.php?adm=inkoop&amp;f=open"><small>Inkoop nog te betalen</small><strong><?= h(adm_eur($open)) ?></strong><span><?= $openN ?> <?= $openN === 1 ? 'factuur' : 'facturen' ?></span></a>
      <a class="adm-stat" href="admin.php?adm=inkoop&amp;f=controleren"><small>Nog controleren</small><strong><?= $contr ?></strong><span>Uit de pdf gelezen, kijk even na</span></a>
      <a class="adm-stat" href="admin.php?adm=mailbox"><small>Mailbox</small><strong style="font-size:18px;margin-top:8px"><?= $gekoppeld ? 'Gekoppeld' : 'Niet gekoppeld' ?></strong><span><?= $gekoppeld ? ($m['laatst'] !== '' ? 'Opgehaald ' . h(date('d-m H:i', strtotime($m['laatst']))) : 'Nog niet opgehaald') : 'Koppel hem, dan komen facturen vanzelf binnen' ?></span></a>
    </div>
    <?php if ($m['fout'] !== ''): ?><div class="err">Mail ophalen lukte de laatste keer niet: <?= h($m['fout']) ?> <a href="admin.php?adm=mailbox" style="color:#fff">Mailbox controleren</a></div><?php endif; ?>
    <?php if ($m['kandidaten']): ?><div class="ok">Er <?= count($m['kandidaten']) === 1 ? 'is 1 afzender' : 'zijn ' . count($m['kandidaten']) . ' afzenders' ?> met pdf-bijlagen die nog niet in je lijst <?= count($m['kandidaten']) === 1 ? 'staat' : 'staan' ?>. <a href="admin.php?adm=mailbox#kandidaten" style="color:#8fb3ff;font-weight:600">Bekijk ze</a></div><?php endif; ?>
    <div class="adm-chips">
      <?php foreach (['' => 'Alle', 'open' => 'Open', 'telaat' => 'Te laat', 'betaald' => 'Betaald', 'genegeerd' => 'Genegeerd', 'controleren' => 'Controleren'] as $k => $l): ?><a class="<?= $f === $k ? 'on' : '' ?>" href="admin.php?adm=inkoop<?= $k !== '' ? '&amp;f=' . $k : '' ?><?= $q !== '' ? '&amp;k=' . h($q) : '' ?>"><?= h($l) ?></a><?php endforeach; ?>
      <form method="get"><input type="hidden" name="adm" value="inkoop" /><?php if ($f !== ''): ?><input type="hidden" name="f" value="<?= h($f) ?>" /><?php endif; ?><select name="k" onchange="this.form.submit()" style="width:auto;padding:7px 10px;font-size:14px"><option value="">Alle kwartalen</option><?php foreach (array_keys($kwartalen) as $kw): list($j, $n) = explode('-', $kw); ?><option value="<?= h($kw) ?>"<?= $q === $kw ? ' selected' : '' ?>>Kwartaal <?= $n ?> <?= $j ?></option><?php endforeach; ?></select></form>
    </div>
    <div class="adm-acties">
      <a class="btn sm" href="admin.php?adm=ink">+ Inkoopfactuur</a>
      <?php if ($gekoppeld): ?><form method="post"><input type="hidden" name="action" value="adm_mail_halen" /><button class="btn ghost sm" type="submit">Mail ophalen</button></form><?php endif; ?>
      <a class="btn ghost sm" href="admin.php?adm=mailbox">Mailbox en afzenders</a>
      <a class="btn ghost sm" href="admin.php?adm=export&amp;wat=inkoop&amp;jaar=<?= h(date('Y')) ?>">Download inkoop (Excel)</a>
    </div>
    <?php
    $rijen = array_filter($lijst, function($x) use ($f, $q) {
        $s = adm_ink_status($x)[0];
        if ($f === 'controleren' && !(!empty($x['controleren']) && $x['status'] === 'open')) return false;
        if (in_array($f, ['open', 'telaat', 'betaald', 'genegeerd'], true) && !($s === $f || ($f === 'open' && $s === 'telaat'))) return false;
        if ($q !== '' && substr($x['datum'], 0, 4) . '-' . (int)ceil((int)substr($x['datum'], 5, 2) / 3) !== $q) return false;
        return true;
    });
    if (!$rijen) { echo '<p class="adm-leeg">' . ($lijst ? 'Niets in deze selectie.' : 'Nog geen inkoopfacturen. Koppel je mailbox of voeg er een toe met + Inkoopfactuur.') . '</p>'; return; }
    echo '<div class="adm-scroll"><table class="adm-tab"><thead><tr><th class="mob-weg">Datum</th><th>Leverancier</th><th class="r">Bedrag</th><th class="r mob-weg">Btw</th><th>Status</th><th class="mob-weg"></th></tr></thead><tbody>';
    foreach ($rijen as $x) {
        $s = adm_ink_status($x); $u = 'admin.php?adm=ink&id=' . rawurlencode($x['id']);
        $btw = $x['btwsoort'] === 'verlegd' ? 'verlegd' : ($x['btwsoort'] === 'geen' ? 'geen' : adm_eur($x['btw']));
        echo '<tr class="klik" onclick="location.href=\'' . h($u) . '\'"><td class="mob-weg">' . h(date('d-m-Y', strtotime($x['datum']))) . '</td>'
           . '<td><a href="' . h($u) . '">' . h($x['leverancier']) . '</a><span class="sub2">' . h(trim(($x['nummer'] !== '' ? 'Nr. ' . $x['nummer'] : '') . ($x['oms'] !== '' ? '  ' . cut($x['oms'], 50) : ''))) . '</span>'
           . '<span class="sub2 mob-ja">' . h(date('d-m-Y', strtotime($x['datum']))) . '</span>'
           . (!empty($x['controleren']) && $x['status'] === 'open' ? '<span class="st st-concept" style="margin-top:4px">Controleren</span>' : '') . '</td>'
           . '<td class="r">' . h(adm_eur($x['bedrag'])) . '</td><td class="r mob-weg">' . h($btw) . '</td>'
           . '<td><span class="st st-' . ($s[0] === 'open' ? 'open' : ($s[0] === 'telaat' ? 'telaat' : ($s[0] === 'betaald' ? 'betaald' : 'concept'))) . '">' . h($s[1]) . '</span></td><td class="mob-weg r" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">'
           . ($x['pdf'] !== '' ? '<a class="btn ghost sm" href="admin.php?adm=pdf&amp;id=' . h($x['id']) . '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">PDF</a> ' : '')
           . ($s[0] === 'open' || $s[0] === 'telaat' ? '<form method="post" style="display:inline"><input type="hidden" name="action" value="adm_ink_status" /><input type="hidden" name="id" value="' . h($x['id']) . '" /><input type="hidden" name="status" value="betaald" /><button class="btn ghost sm" type="submit">Betaald</button></form>' : '')
           . '</td></tr>';
    }
    echo '</tbody></table></div>';
}
function adm_v_ink() {
    $lijst = adm_inkoop();
    $id = (string)($_GET['id'] ?? '');
    $i = $id !== '' ? adm_vind($lijst, $id) : null;
    if ($id !== '' && $i === null) { echo '<p class="adm-leeg">Deze inkoopfactuur bestaat niet (meer). <a href="admin.php?adm=inkoop">Terug</a></p>'; return; }
    $x = $i !== null ? $lijst[$i] : ['id' => '', 'leverancier' => '', 'nummer' => '', 'datum' => date('Y-m-d'), 'termijn' => 30, 'bedrag' => 0, 'btw' => 0, 'btwsoort' => 'nl', 'oms' => '', 'status' => 'open', 'pdf' => '', 'controleren' => false, 'bron' => 'hand'];
    $s = $i !== null ? adm_ink_status($x) : ['open', ''];
    ?>
    <h2 style="font-size:22px"><?= $i !== null ? 'Inkoopfactuur ' . h($x['leverancier']) : 'Nieuwe inkoopfactuur' ?> <?php if ($i !== null): ?><span class="st st-<?= $s[0] === 'telaat' ? 'telaat' : ($s[0] === 'betaald' ? 'betaald' : ($s[0] === 'open' ? 'open' : 'concept')) ?>"><?= h($s[1]) ?></span><?php endif; ?></h2>
    <?php if ($i === null): ?><p class="sub">Kies de pdf. De admin leest datum, bedrag en btw dan zelf uit de factuur, en jij controleert het.</p><?php endif; ?>
    <?php if ($x['pdf'] !== '' && isset($x['leesbaar']) && !$x['leesbaar'] && $x['status'] === 'open') { adm_ocr_js($x['id']); } elseif (!empty($x['controleren']) && $x['status'] === 'open') { ?><div class="ok">Deze gegevens zijn uit de pdf gelezen. Kijk ze na en klik op Opslaan.</div><?php } ?>
    <div class="adm-ink">
      <form class="formcard" method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data" style="margin:0">
